\section{0. Abstract and Epistemic
Scope}\label{abstract-and-epistemic-scope}

Collective agency across living, cultural, and computational systems
scales whenever individual participants externalize structured state
into a shared medium, neighboring participants ingest that signal across
a selective threshold or verification gate, and the resulting state
transition locks in useful work without regressing to prior noise.
Across 3.8 billion years of biological evolution, major jumps in
collective capability repeatedly coincided with new intercellular or
inter-organism communication substrates rather than raw increases in
isolated cell size: primordial membrane vesicles, cooperative bacterial
quorum switches, calcium-gated cyanobacterial nanopores, dictyostelid
cyclic nucleotide waves, choanoflagellate electrical calcium transients,
metazoan gap junctions, stigmergic trails, topological flocks,
compositional human language, writing, print, and packet-switched
networks. Contemporary artificial intelligence instantiates a digital
counterpart in sparse mixture-of-experts architectures and multi-agent
verification loops, while emerging neural and paracrine technologies
point toward a future, still-speculative bidirectional analog
counterpart.

This study formalizes, simulates, and audits that cross-scale thesis
along two concrete deliverables: computational orchestration efficiency
(quantified by coherence per joule) and candidate neurological
supplementation pathways. Mechanically verified proofs in Lean 4.36.0
(45 theorems across QuorumRatchet, OrchestrationFlow, and
EnzymaticSynthesis, zero incomplete placeholders, zero custom axioms)
establish non-regression of gated externalization ratchets, zero-clock
sub-threshold idle conservation, cooperative Hill noise suppression,
septal nanopore fault containment, joule dominance of
deterministic-first model escalation, successive-shortest-path
reduced-cost optimality, and preservation of individual autonomy
invariants under opt-in multi-agent bridging. Pre-registered
deterministic simulations (seed \texttt{20261123}) quantify bacterial
quorum metabolic savings (\texttt{95.49\%} to \texttt{99.17\%} regulon
energy saved at low density), cyanobacterial filament survival
(\texttt{95.0\%} viable with rapid pore closure versus \texttt{0.0\%}
ungated), three-dimensional topological flocking cohesion
(\texttt{100.0\%} connected versus \texttt{0.68\%} to \texttt{4.30\%}
for fixed metric radius after fourfold expansion), adaptive plasmodial
shortest-path pruning (\texttt{100.0\%} flux concentration for
superlinear feedback), and 20-generation cultural retention after
synthetic demonstrators are removed (\texttt{100.0\%} optimal retention
with attested externalized artifacts versus \texttt{29.27\%} under
unverified imitation). Across five hardware and operating-system
archetypes spanning physical Texas Instruments whole-board and
rail-separated current shunts on a Tegra \texttt{aarch64} developer kit
(\texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ true}, \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} =
\texttt{927.30} microjoules per \texttt{1}-kibibyte edge on
processor-and-memory rail) and Running Average Power Limit (RAPL),
Darwin kernel, and graphics-driver energy counters across four Linux,
macOS, and Windows 11 workstations (\texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}), compiled Rust
cryptographic hashing (BLAKE3) plus \texttt{Ed25519} channel attestation
verifies envelopes in \texttt{25.95} to \texttt{37.39} microseconds
(\texttt{643} to \texttt{926} microjoules per edge across \texttt{64}
bytes to \texttt{64} kibibytes), zero-clock \texttt{eventfd} thresholded
nodes use \texttt{2.21x} less socket energy than \texttt{1,000}-hertz
clocked polling at \texttt{1\%} bursty duty (\texttt{136.32\ J} versus
\texttt{300.73\ J}, \texttt{2.58\ ms} versus \texttt{570.\allowbreak{}38\ ms} at
\texttt{10\ Hz}), and on a pre-registered 60-task Python code-repair
benchmark (\texttt{240} episodes) the heterogeneous attested
orchestrator matches a monolithic frontier model's \texttt{59\ /\ 60}
(\texttt{98.33\%}) pass rate with \texttt{0\ /\ 60} (\texttt{0.00\%})
false commits at \texttt{432.75\ J} local socket \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}
and \texttt{21} model calls (\texttt{0.136} tasks per joule,
\texttt{2.09x} higher coherence per joule than the \texttt{902.51\ J}
monolithic baseline and \texttt{14.97x} higher than an unattested
four-agent cascade that degraded to \texttt{52\ /\allowbreak{}\ 60\ =\ 86.\allowbreak{}67\%} with
\texttt{8\ /\allowbreak{}\ 60\ =\ 13.\allowbreak{}33\%} false commits), while containing
\texttt{200\ /\allowbreak{}\ 200} (\texttt{100.0\%}) injected Byzantine channel
faults and recomputing min-cost failover routes in \texttt{2.40}
microseconds. Mapping \texttt{38} historical artificial intelligence
milestones from \texttt{1950} to \texttt{2026} onto the same three-phase
ratchet diagnoses both historical winters and unifies ten competing
paradigm silos via sub-millijoule Digital Enzymes (\texttt{78,855x}
faster and \texttt{75,374x} lower physical shunt \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}
than natural-language agent handoffs): across a \texttt{100}-task
five-regime benchmark where every isolated solo paradigm stalls at or
below \texttt{49\%} (\texttt{48\%} single-model, \texttt{49\%}
three-agent debate), immediate ten-camp Digital Enzymatic Synthesis
reaches \texttt{98\ /\allowbreak{}\ 100} (\texttt{98.0\%}) with \texttt{1\%} false
commits and \texttt{9.26x} to \texttt{26.87x} higher physical-rail
coherence per joule, anchoring a falsifiable month, quarter, year, and
decade downward-compilation blueprint. Across 18 neural interface
modalities and 16 primary extracellular-vesicle studies, strict
separation is enforced between replicated preclinical animal rescue and
unverified human clinical efficacy.

\subsection{Measurement and Evidence Class
Ledger}\label{measurement-and-evidence-class-ledger}

\textbf{Roadmap.} Section 1 defines the conserved three-phase
externalization operator and Coherence per Joule (\texttt{CpJ}). Section
2 audits the 3.8-billion-year biological ladder (\texttt{CL01} to
\texttt{CL06}). Section 3 examines cross-species stigmergy, topological
flocks, mycorrhizal evidence versus citation-bias critiques
(\texttt{CL07} to \texttt{CL13}), and pre-registered models
\texttt{CA-M1} to \texttt{CA-M5}. Section 4 traces the human cultural
ratchet, extended mind, sparse \texttt{MoE} foundation models, and
hybrid machine-human cultural transmission (\texttt{CL14} to
\texttt{CL19}). Section 5 presents the event-driven attested
orchestration stack, the 60-task code-repair benchmark (\texttt{CTA-E1}
to \texttt{CTA-E3}), the five-host cross-silicon energy audit including
physical Tegra DC rail shunts (\texttt{CTA-E4}), and the 100-task
ten-paradigm benchmark (\texttt{CTA-E5}, \texttt{CL20}). Section 6
formalizes Digital Enzymatic Engineering across the ten siloed AI camps
(\texttt{C1} to \texttt{C10}), the 5-loop downward-compilation forcing
function, and the \texttt{Month\ /\allowbreak{}\ Quarter\ /\allowbreak{}\ Year\ /\allowbreak{}\ Decade} roadmap
(\texttt{HM01} to \texttt{HD04}). Section 7 details all 45 Lean 4.36.0
theorems (\texttt{CTA-T1} to \texttt{CTA-T45}). Section 8 maps 18
\texttt{BCI} and paracrine modalities (\texttt{BM01} to \texttt{BM18})
alongside 16 primary secretome/\texttt{EV} studies (\texttt{EV01} to
\texttt{EV16}). Section 9 registers ten falsifiable hypotheses
(\texttt{CA-H1} to \texttt{CA-H10}), explicit scope limits, and
concluding invariants. Companion Open Interface Engineering monographs
(\href{https://research.openie.dev/papers/agency/}{Thermodynamic Bounds
and an Optimal Tempo}, framing the OpenIE authors' proposed Universal
Law of Agency; \href{https://research.openie.dev/papers/gates/}{Metered
Commit Gates};
\href{https://research.openie.dev/papers/deterministic-null/}{Deterministic
Null}; and \href{https://research.openie.dev/papers/lut-adapt/}{Downward
LUT6 Compilation}) are cited with \texttt{{[}same\ house{]}} markers
strictly for architectural continuity {[}44{]}.

Theoretical, formal (\texttt{CTA-T1} to \texttt{CTA-T45}), and
biophysical-model (\texttt{CA-M1} to \texttt{CA-M5}) sections carry zero
hardware power claims, whereas Section 5 (\texttt{CTA-E4} and

\section{1. Thesis and the Conserved Externalize-Ingest-Transform
Operator}\label{thesis-and-the-conserved-externalize-ingest-transform-operator}

Across biological evolution, cultural history, and distributed
computation, isolated agent capacity encounters steep metabolic and
thermodynamic diminishing returns long before collective task complexity
saturates. What repeatedly unlocks superlinear capability is a
\textbf{communication tool avalanche}: a conserved three-phase operator
in which agents write internal state into an external physical
substrate, neighboring agents selectively ingest that substrate only
when an activation threshold and validity gate are satisfied, and
ingested signals transform recipient state while preserving prior
verified gains (\texttt{Figure\ 1}).

Let a population of \(N\) agents carry local states
\(s_i(t) \in \mathcal{S}\) (\(i \in \{1,\dots,N\}\)) coupled to a shared
physical or digital medium \(\mathcal{M}\). Each rung of the avalanche
instantiates three maps (\texttt{{[}T{]}}, formalized in
\texttt{Avalanche.\allowbreak{}QuorumRatchet.\allowbreak{}CycleOperator}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Externalization (\(\mathcal{E} : \mathcal{S} \to \Sigma\))}:
  Agent \(i\) expends bounded signaling energy \(e_{\text{ext}} > 0\) to
  emit a structured physical artifact or packet \(m = \mathcal{E}(s_i)\)
  into \(\mathcal{M}\) (an acyl-homoserine lactone molecule, a slime
  sheath, an acoustic contour, a printed equation, or a
  \texttt{BLAKE3}-hashed, \texttt{Ed25519}-signed channel payload).
\item
  \textbf{Thresholded and Gated Ingestion
  (\(\mathcal{G} : \mathcal{S} \times \Sigma^* \to \{0,1\}\))}:
  Recipient \(j\) accumulates weighted incoming signals
  \(A_j = \sum_k w_{kj}\,m_k\) and fires if and only if
  \(A_j \ge \theta_j\) and receptor/verifier specificity
  \(V_j(m_k) = 1\) holds:
  \[s_j(t + 1) = \begin{cases} \mathcal{T}\!\left(s_j(t),\, \{m_k\}\right) & \text{if } \sum_k w_{kj}\,\mathbb{I}[V_j(m_k)=1] \ge \theta_j, \\ s_j(t) & \text{otherwise.} \end{cases}\]
  Sub-threshold arrivals (\(A_j < \theta_j\)) and unverified payloads
  (\(V_j(m_k) = 0\)) trigger zero downstream state transitions
  (\texttt{CTA-T7}, \texttt{CTA-T8}, \texttt{CTA-T17},
  \texttt{CTA-T18}).
\item
  \textbf{Ratchet Transformation and Downward Crystallization
  (\(\mathcal{T} : \mathcal{S} \times \Sigma^* \to \mathcal{S}\))}:
  Accepted signals update both collective coordination and individual
  competence. When validated artifacts persist in \(\mathcal{M}\) with
  quality non-regression \(Q(s_j(t+1)) \ge Q(s_j(t))\), high-energy
  exploratory discoveries become low-cost reusable templates for
  subsequent generations (\texttt{CTA-T5}, \texttt{CTA-T6},
  \texttt{CTA-T23}).
\end{enumerate}

Without a selective gate \(V_j\), a relay chain of \(k\) hops with
per-hop fidelity \(f \in (0, 1)\) decays exponentially as \(f^k\)
(\texttt{CTA-T2}), and additive noise \(\epsilon > 0\) grows linearly as
\(k\epsilon\) (\texttt{CTA-T1}). With a sound verification gate and
persistent externalized storage, quality becomes a monotonically
non-decreasing ratchet (\texttt{CTA-T5}). To compare coordination
architectures across biological and silicon substrates on equal footing,
let \(Q \ge 0\) denote task-verified coherent output (such as
test-verified bug repairs, retained metabolic viability, or error-free
transmitted bits) and
\(E_{\text{total}} = E_{\text{compute}} + E_{\text{comm}} + E_{\text{idle}} > 0\)
denote total expended energy. \textbf{Coherence per Joule} is defined
as:

\[\mathrm{CpJ} = \frac{Q}{E_{\text{compute}} + E_{\text{comm}} + E_{\text{idle}}}\;\; \left[\text{verified coherence units} \cdot \mathrm{J}^{-1}\right].\]

Raw component count (\texttt{5,570} bacterial open reading frames,
\(8.6 \times 10^{10}\) human neurons, or \(2 \times 10^{12}\) frontier
neural-network weights) sets only an upper capacity envelope; realized
\(\mathrm{CpJ}\) is governed by whether communication channels are
thresholded, event-driven, and gated against downstream error cascades
(\texttt{CTA-T19} to \texttt{CTA-T23}).

& Tissue-scale epithelial and neuromuscular sheets (\(10^5\) to

\texttt{Ca2+} & Loss of high-\texttt{Ca2+} junctional uncoupling failing

Three quantitative lessons emerge directly from the primary biological
record (\texttt{{[}E{]}}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Thresholded public-good gating (\texttt{CL02})}: First
  characterized via autoinduction of bioluminescence in \emph{Vibrio
  fischeri} by Nealson, Platt, and Hastings (1970, 1979) {[}42{]} and
  surveyed across Gram-negative and Gram-positive circuits by Miller and
  Bassler (2001) {[}37{]}, quorum sensing withholds metabolically
  expensive secreted virulence factors, siderophores, elastases, and
  biofilms until extracellular autoinducer concentration crosses a
  cooperative dissociation threshold \(K\). Microarray transcriptomics
  in \emph{Pseudomonas aeruginosa} by Schuster et al.~(2003) {[}37{]}
  demonstrated that \texttt{315} to \texttt{\textgreater{}500} genes
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}6\%} to \texttt{10\%} of its
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}5,570} \texttt{ORFs}) are regulated by the
  \texttt{LasR}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{RhlR} acyl-homoserine lactone cascade. Rather
  than polling a central clock, cells integrate chemical concentration
  gradients locally.
\item
  \textbf{Physical circuit-breakers make multicellular sharing
  survivable (\texttt{CL03}, \texttt{CL06})}: Sharing cytoplasm across
  cells creates an immediate vulnerability: localized predation or
  membrane rupture would drain the entire filament unless junctions
  close upon damage. Early Archaean stromatolite reefs
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}3.43\ Ga}, Allwood et al.~2006) {[}1{]}
  attest to ancient microbial mat organization, and cryo-electron
  tomography of \emph{Anabaena} septal junctions by Weiss et al.~(2019)
  {[}49{]} alongside divisome/septal characterization of \texttt{SepJ}
  and \texttt{SepI} (Flores et al.~2018; Springstein et al.~2020)
  {[}24{]} revealed a proteinaceous nanopore tube-cap-plug assembly
  functionally convergent with metazoan pannexin/connexin gap junctions
  (Panchin et al.~2000) {[}45{]}. Stress or ionophore-induced
  \texttt{Ca2+} influx rearranges the cap-plug module to seal the pore
  reversibly, isolating lysed cells while preserving nitrogen-fixing
  heterocyst metabolite exchange across intact neighbors
  (\texttt{CTA-T15}).
\item
  \textbf{Multicellularity reuses intracellular compute while inventing
  externalized I/O (\texttt{CL04}, \texttt{CL05})}: Sequencing
  \emph{Dictyostelium discoideum} (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}12,500}
  genes, Eichinger et al.~2005) {[}19{]} and comparing genomes across
  all major dictyostelid clades (Glockner et al.~2016) {[}26{]} showed
  that roughly \texttt{80\%} of developmentally essential genes predate
  multicellularity in solitary amoebozoan relatives; the
  lineage-specific \texttt{\textasciitilde{}20\%} are heavily enriched
  in secreted and transmembrane recognition/signaling proteins.
  Likewise, in choanoflagellates (the closest unicellular and colonial
  sister group to animals), Brunet et al.~(2019) {[}8{]} discovered
  light-regulated collective sheet inversion in \emph{Choanoeca flexa},
  and Colgren and Burkhardt (2025) {[}15{]} demonstrated in
  \emph{Salpingoeca rosetta} that voltage-gated calcium channels
  (\texttt{VGCCs}) propagate colony-wide electrical/calcium waves
  coordinating synchronous ciliary arrest and contraction before the
  evolutionary appearance of synapses.
\end{enumerate}

\section{\texorpdfstring{3. Cross-Species Stigmergy, Mycorrhizal
Critique, and Executed Models (\texttt{CA-M1} to
\texttt{CA-M5})}{3. Cross-Species Stigmergy, Mycorrhizal Critique, and Executed Models (CA-M1 to CA-M5)}}\label{cross-species-stigmergy-mycorrhizal-critique-and-executed-models-ca-m1-to-ca-m5}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{3.1 Cross-species decentralized coordination
and candid evidence boundaries (\texttt{CL07} to
\texttt{CL13})}{3.1 Cross-species decentralized coordination and candid evidence boundaries (CL07 to CL13)}}\label{cross-species-decentralized-coordination-and-candid-evidence-boundaries-cl07-to-cl13}

Beyond tissue junctions, free-moving organisms externalize spatial and
kinematic state directly into environmental fields (\texttt{{[}E{]}}):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Stigmergy and plasmodial externalized memory (\texttt{CL08},
  \texttt{CL10})}: Grasse (1959) and Theraulaz and Bonabeau (1999)
  {[}27{]} showed in social insects (\emph{Bellicositermes},
  \emph{Cubitermes}) that architecture itself serves as the
  communication channel: workers respond to local pheromone-impregnated
  soil curvature without a blueprint. In the aneural syncytial slime
  mold \emph{Physarum polycephalum}, Nakagaki, Yamada, and Toth (2000)
  {[}40{]} demonstrated shortest-path maze solving, Tero et al.~(2010)
  {[}40{]} showed that adaptive Poiseuille protoplasmic tubes match the
  cost, transport efficiency, and fault tolerance of the Tokyo rail
  network, and Reid et al.~(2012) {[}40{]} proved that \emph{Physarum}
  deposits an extracellular non-living slime mat functioning as
  externalized spatial memory to escape U-shaped traps, failing when
  placed on pre-slime-coated agar.
\item
  \textbf{Symbolic vector dances, topological flocks, signature
  whistles, and collective electrosensing (\texttt{CL09}, \texttt{CL11},
  \texttt{CL12}, \texttt{CL13})}: Honeybees (\emph{Apis mellifera})
  encode floral distance and solar azimuth into waggle-run duration and
  angle (von Frisch 1967) {[}48{]}. Stereoscopic 3D reconstructions of
  starling (\emph{Sturnus vulgaris}) murmurations by Ballerini et
  al.~(2008) {[}3{]} and Cavagna et al.~(2010) {[}11{]} revealed that
  each bird tracks a \textbf{topological} neighborhood of
  \texttt{k\ \textasciitilde{}\ 6\ to\ 7} nearest neighbors regardless
  of flock density, yielding scale-free velocity-fluctuation
  correlations whose correlation length grows linearly with flock size
  (\(\xi \propto L\), \(\xi / L \approx 0.35\)). Bottlenose dolphins
  (\emph{Tursiops truncatus}) broadcast and vocally copy individualized
  frequency-modulated signature whistles that operate as referential
  identity labels independent of voice timbre (Caldwell and Caldwell
  1965; Janik and Sayigh 2013) {[}10, 30{]}. Schooling fish combine
  \texttt{\textless{}10\ ms} lateral-line hydrodynamic coupling
  (Partridge and Pitcher 1980) with collective active sensing: Pedraja
  and Sawtell (2024) {[}46{]} showed in weakly electric
  \emph{Gnathonemus petersii} that conspecific electric organ discharges
  (\texttt{EODs}) illuminate shadowed objects and extend electrolocation
  range twofold to threefold.
\item
  \textbf{Mycorrhizal fungal transfer versus mother-tree
  overinterpretation (\texttt{CL07})}: Using reciprocal
  \(^{13}\mathrm{C}\) and \(^{14}\mathrm{C}\) pulse labeling in the
  field, Simard et al.~(1997) {[}48{]} demonstrated bidirectional carbon
  transfer between \emph{Betula papyrifera} and \emph{Pseudotsuga
  menziesii} seedlings linked by ectomycorrhizal hyphae, with net
  transfer modulated by shading. However, scientific honesty requires
  pairing that finding with the systematic audit by Karst, Jones, and
  Hoeksema (2023) {[}32{]}, who showed across the forestry literature
  that positive citation bias and uncontrolled confounding factors (root
  grafts, soil diffusion, fungal-owned carbon retention, and seedling
  mortality) do \textbf{not} support popular claims of intentional
  ``mother-tree'' kin altruism or teleological warning broadcasts. In
  our framework, mycorrhizal transfer remains a genuine source-sink
  biophysical coupling (\texttt{{[}E{]}} for hyphal resource flux),
  while adaptive maternal signaling intent is classified as
  unsubstantiated (\texttt{{[}S{]}} / contested).
\end{itemize}

Notably, \texttt{CA-M4} highlights an exact dynamical boundary: because
\(f(Q) = Q^\mu / (1 + Q^\mu)\) has an inflection point at
\(Q_{\text{infl}} = ((\mu - 1)/(\mu + 1))^{1/\mu}\) for \(\mu > 1\),
superlinear shortest-path selection operates strictly inside the
unsaturated biological flux window (\(|Q| < Q_{\text{infl}}\));
overdriving source flux into saturation converts \(f(Q)\) into a concave
plateau that preserves parallel redundant tubes.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 1: Biophysical Threshold
Economics --- Why a Lone Secreting Bacterium Goes Bankrupt and Why
\texttt{SepJ} Must Seal in \texttt{\textless{}=\ 0.30\ s}
(\texttt{CA-M1}, \texttt{CA-M2},
\texttt{CTA-T11}--\texttt{CTA-T15})}{Worked Example 1: Biophysical Threshold Economics --- Why a Lone Secreting Bacterium Goes Bankrupt and Why SepJ Must Seal in \textless= 0.30 s (CA-M1, CA-M2, CTA-T11--CTA-T15)}}\label{worked-example-1-biophysical-threshold-economics-why-a-lone-secreting-bacterium-goes-bankrupt-and-why-sepj-must-seal-in-0.30-s-ca-m1-ca-m2-cta-t11cta-t15}

To see why biological externalization requires both \textbf{cooperative
activation thresholds} and \textbf{rapid fault-containment gates}, trace
the exact adenosine triphosphate (\texttt{ATP}) and metabolite equations
executed in \texttt{CA-M1} and \texttt{CA-M2}
(\texttt{seed\ =\ 20261123}, \texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ false},
\texttt{{[}M{]}}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Phase 1 (Bounded Tracer Externalization vs.~Full Secretome
  Cost)}: Consider a model bacterium with \texttt{5,000} open reading
  frames (\texttt{ORFs}), matching the genomic scale of
  \emph{Pseudomonas aeruginosa} (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}5,570}
  \texttt{ORFs}). Housekeeping metabolism across \texttt{4,400}
  intracellular genes requires \texttt{1.0} normalized \texttt{ATP} unit
  per gene per cell cycle (\texttt{4,\allowbreak{}400.\allowbreak{}0\ ATP}), whereas \texttt{600}
  quorum-regulated genes (\texttt{12.0\%} of the genome: secreted
  elastases, siderophores, biofilm exopolysaccharides, or \emph{Vibrio}
  luciferase) carry a \texttt{3.0x} extracellular synthesis-and-export
  cost (\texttt{1,\allowbreak{}800.\allowbreak{}0\ ATP}). A constitutive mutant that expresses all
  \texttt{5,000} genes unconditionally expends:
  \[E_{\text{const}} = 4{,}400.0 + 1{,}800.0 = 6{,}200.0\;\text{ATP/cycle},\]
  burning \texttt{29.03\%} (\texttt{1,\allowbreak{}800\ /\allowbreak{}\ 6,\allowbreak{}200}) of its entire
  cellular energy budget on exported macromolecules that advective
  wash-out dilutes below functional concentration whenever local cell
  density \(\rho\) sits far below quorum threshold \(\rho_K\). Instead,
  wild-type \texttt{LuxI}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{LasI} externalizes a tiny
  acyl-homoserine lactone (\texttt{AHL}) tracer costing
  \(e_{\text{ext}} = 12.0\;\text{ATP/cycle}\) (\texttt{0.19\%} of
  \(E_{\text{const}}\)).
\item
  \textbf{Phase 2 (Cooperative Hill Gate Under Sub-Threshold Noise)}: At
  planktonic low density \(\rho / \rho_K = 0.20\) (extracellular
  autoinducer ratio \(A / K = 0.20\) at diffusivity loss \texttt{0.50}),
  the rational Hill receptor switch
  \(H_{n_H}(A/K) = (A/K)^{n_H} / [1 + (A/K)^{n_H}]\) (\texttt{CTA-T11}
  to \texttt{CTA-T14}) yields starkly different metabolic budgets across
  cooperativity exponents \(n_H \in \{1, 2, 4\}\):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Monomeric receptor (\(n_H = 1\))}:
    \(H_1(0.20) = 0.20 / 1.20 = 1/6 \approx 0.1667\), spending
    \(E_{\text{reg}}(1) = 12.0 + 1{,}800 \times (1/6) = 312.0\;\text{ATP}\)
    (\texttt{4,\allowbreak{}712.\allowbreak{}0\ ATP} total). Worse, when diffusive micro-eddies
    inject sub-threshold concentration fluctuations
    (\(\bar{A}/K = 0.25\), Gaussian \(\sigma = 0.25\,K\) across
    \texttt{2,000} trials), \(H_1\) suffers premature regulon firing
    (\(H_1 > 0.20\)) on \textbf{\texttt{51.90\%}}
    (\texttt{1,\allowbreak{}038\ /\allowbreak{}\ 2,\allowbreak{}000}) of cycles.
  \item
    \textbf{Cooperative dimer (\(n_H = 2\), \texttt{LuxR}-class)}:
    \(H_2(0.20) = 0.04 / 1.04 = 1/26 \approx 0.03846\), slashing regulon
    spend to
    \(E_{\text{reg}}(2) = 12.0 + 69.231 = \mathbf{81.231\;\text{ATP}}\)
    (\textbf{\texttt{95.49\%} regulon \texttt{ATP} saved},
    \texttt{27.72\%} whole-cell \texttt{ATP} saved at
    \texttt{4,\allowbreak{}481.\allowbreak{}23\ ATP}) and cutting premature noise wakes
    \texttt{2.92x} down to \textbf{\texttt{17.75\%}}.
  \item
    \textbf{Cooperative tetramer (\(n_H = 4\))}:
    \(H_4(0.20) = 0.0016 / 1.0016 \approx 0.001597\), dropping regulon
    spend to
    \(E_{\text{reg}}(4) = 12.0 + 2.875 = \mathbf{14.875\;\text{ATP}}\)
    (\textbf{\texttt{99.17\%} regulon \texttt{ATP} saved},
    \texttt{28.79\%} whole-cell \texttt{ATP} saved at
    \texttt{4,\allowbreak{}414.\allowbreak{}88\ ATP}) and cutting false wakes \texttt{11.66x} down
    to \textbf{\texttt{4.45\%}} (\texttt{CTA-T13}).
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Phase 3 (Multicellular \texttt{SepJ} Circuit-Breaker Timing)}:
  Once cells couple directly via \texttt{\textasciitilde{}15\ nm}
  cyanobacterial septal nanopores (\texttt{CA-M2}, \(L = 20\) coupled
  cells integrated via fourth-order Runge-Kutta \texttt{RK4} at
  \(\Delta t = 0.002\;\text{s}\) over \texttt{20.0\ s}), terminal
  \texttt{cell\ 0} membrane lysis at \(t = 0\) creates an immediate
  cytoplasmic sink. If \(\text{Ca}^{2+}\)-triggered \texttt{SepJ}
  cap-plug closure completes in
  \(\tau_{\text{close}} = 0.05\;\text{s}\), \texttt{junction\ (\allowbreak{}0,\allowbreak{}1)}
  seals to \texttt{0.000} open fraction, preserving \texttt{90.48\%} of
  \texttt{cell\ 1}'s metabolite pool (\texttt{93.86\%} at distal
  \texttt{cell\ 19}) and \textbf{\texttt{19\ /\ 20} (\texttt{95.0\%})}
  viable cells (\texttt{87.56\%} total filament capacity). At
  \(\tau_{\text{close}} \in [0.15\;\text{s}, 0.30\;\text{s}]\),
  \texttt{cell\ 1} absorbs a steep transient drop (\texttt{17.66\%} to
  \texttt{13.19\%} metabolite) yet seals fast enough to save
  \textbf{\texttt{18\ /\ 20} (\texttt{90.0\%})} cells (\texttt{85.11\%}
  to \texttt{77.85\%} capacity). Slowing closure past \texttt{0.30\ s}
  triggers progressive bystander collapse (\texttt{16\ /\allowbreak{}\ 20\ =\ 80.\allowbreak{}0\%}
  at \texttt{1.20\ s}; \texttt{5\ /\allowbreak{}\ 20\ =\ 25.\allowbreak{}0\%} at
  \texttt{5.00\ s}), and an ungated lock-open mutant drains the entire
  filament to \textbf{\texttt{0\ /\ 20} (\texttt{0.0\%})} viable cells
  (\texttt{14.28\%} residual pool, \texttt{CTA-T15}).
\end{enumerate}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{\texorpdfstring{4. Human Cultural Ratchet, Extended Mind,
Machine Culture, and the Digital Hive Mind (\texttt{CL14} to
\texttt{CL19})}{4. Human Cultural Ratchet, Extended Mind, Machine Culture, and the Digital Hive Mind (CL14 to CL19)}}\label{human-cultural-ratchet-extended-mind-machine-culture-and-the-digital-hive-mind-cl14-to-cl19}

Human cognitive evolution scaled collective output without rewriting
cortical neuron biophysics by progressively lowering signal loss and
decoupling artifact lifetime from individual mortality
(\texttt{{[}E{]}}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Shared intentionality, externalized ledgers, and cumulative
  culture (\texttt{CL14} to \texttt{CL17})}: Against an adult human
  brain power budget of roughly \texttt{20\ W} (Balasubramanian 2021)
  {[}2{]}, overt human behavioral throughput averages
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}10\ bit/s} (Zheng and Meister 2025) {[}50{]}
  and spoken natural language across \texttt{17} languages encodes
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}39\ bit/s} (Coupe et al.~2019) {[}17{]}.
  Despite that tight per-person serial bottleneck, shared intentionality
  (Tomasello et al.~2005) and high-fidelity social ratification create
  the \textbf{cultural ratchet} (Tennie, Call, and Tomasello 2009)
  {[}48{]}. Sociological and philosophical accounts framed the resulting
  macro-structures as collective consciousness (Durkheim 1893), the
  noosphere (Teilhard de Chardin 1955) {[}18{]}, the \textbf{extended
  mind} in which external notebooks and symbols form constitutive parts
  of cognitive processing (Clark and Chalmers 1998) {[}14{]}, and global
  stigmergic coordination across packet-switched networks (Heylighen
  2007) {[}28{]}. Writing (\texttt{CL15}), movable-type print and peer
  review (\texttt{CL16}), and content-addressed version control
  (\texttt{Git} + automated \texttt{CI} gates, \texttt{CL17})
  successively replaced lossy oral memory (\(f^k\) multi-hop decay) with
  persistent, inspectable external records.
\item
  \textbf{Sparse \texttt{MoE} foundation models as a digital hive mind
  (\texttt{CL18})}: Frontier neural architectures increasingly separate
  total stored parameter count (reaching \(1 \times 10^{12}\) to
  \(3 \times 10^{12}\) parameters) from active compute per token via
  sparse \texttt{MoE} top-\(k\) routing (Fedus, Zoph, and Shazeer 2022)
  {[}21{]}, activating only \texttt{\textasciitilde{}3\%} to
  \texttt{12\%} of expert weights on any single token, analogous to
  specialized multicellular differentiation (\texttt{{[}same\ house{]}}
  hardware-paged routing in \emph{Downward LUT6 Compilation} {[}44{]}).
  Raw parameter count parallels raw cell count; coordination routing and
  verification govern \(\mathrm{CpJ}\).
\item
  \textbf{Hybrid machine-human cultural ratchets and \texttt{POLIS}
  societies (\texttt{CL19})}: Brinkmann et al.~(2023) {[}6{]} formalized
  \textbf{machine culture}, showing that intelligent machines now act as
  cultural variation generators, selectors, and transmitters. Crucially,
  multi-generation human-machine laboratory experiments by Brinkmann et
  al.~(2026) {[}7{]} demonstrated that when deep reinforcement learning
  agents discovered a counterintuitive non-myopic strategy that
  \texttt{599} of \texttt{600} isolated human participants missed, and
  the machine agents were \textbf{completely removed} after the initial
  generation, prestige-biased human social learning preserved the
  machine-discovered strategy down to the final generation in \texttt{9}
  of \texttt{15} (\texttt{60\%}) hybrid populations, making every
  final-generation participant in those lineages a cultural descendant
  of a removed machine agent (\texttt{CA-M5} reproduces both this
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}60\%-76\%} prestige-imitation persistence and
  its vulnerability to copy noise relative to \texttt{100\%} attested
  artifact libraries). Complementarily, Zhuang (2025) {[}50{]}
  introduced \texttt{POLIS} (\emph{The Ratchet Effect in Silico}),
  showing that micro-societies of \texttt{1B} to \texttt{4B}
  \texttt{LLMs} cycling through generation, peer verification, shared
  cultural memory, and internalization gain \texttt{+8.8} to
  \texttt{+18.9} benchmark points, with mechanistic ablations confirming
  \textbf{peer verification} as the primary anti-regression ratchet
  operator.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{4.4 The 1950--2026 Artificial Intelligence
Ratchet Audit (\texttt{AI01} to
\texttt{AI38})}{4.4 The 1950--2026 Artificial Intelligence Ratchet Audit (AI01 to AI38)}}\label{the-19502026-artificial-intelligence-ratchet-audit-ai01-to-ai38}

Catalog
\texttt{openie-\allowbreak{}research-\allowbreak{}experiments/\allowbreak{}communication-\allowbreak{}avalanche/\allowbreak{}catalogs/\allowbreak{}ai-\allowbreak{}history-\allowbreak{}ratchet-\allowbreak{}1950-\allowbreak{}2026.\allowbreak{}csv}
(\texttt{AI01} to \texttt{AI38}) audits 38 foundational milestones
across 76 years of artificial intelligence (\texttt{1950} to
\texttt{2026}) by projecting each system onto the exact conserved tuple
\texttt{(\allowbreak{}externalized\_\allowbreak{}signal\_\allowbreak{}substrate,\allowbreak{}\ ingestion\_\allowbreak{}routing\_\allowbreak{}rule,\allowbreak{}\ threshold\_\allowbreak{}or\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}gate,\allowbreak{}\ bottleneck\_\allowbreak{}broken,\allowbreak{}\ failure\_\allowbreak{}boundary\_\allowbreak{}or\_\allowbreak{}winter\_\allowbreak{}cause)}
(\texttt{{[}E{]}} for published historical milestones;
\texttt{{[}T{]}}/\texttt{{[}M{]}} for 2026 downward-compilation
syntheses). Viewed through that structural lens, the history of machine
intelligence is neither a sequence of unrelated architectural fashions
nor a smooth homogeneous curve of dense floating-point throughput. Every
lasting inflection unlocked a \textbf{new externalized communication
channel paired with a selective admission or conservation gate}, and
both \textbf{AI Winters} struck precisely when externalized state
proliferated without a tractable search prior or sound verification
gate:


\begin{center}
\openiefig{fig/communication-avalanche-fig04-ai-history-ratchet-1950-2026.png}{0.94}{https://research.openie.dev/figures/communication-avalanche/ai-history-ratchet-1950-2026.svg}
\end{center}


\emph{\textbf{Figure 4.} Seventy-six-year artificial intelligence
ratchet audit (\texttt{AI01} to \texttt{AI38}, \texttt{1950-2026}).
Every upward step pairs an externalized representation with a selective
threshold or verifier gate; \textbf{AI Winter I} (\texttt{1974-1980})
and \textbf{AI Winter II} (\texttt{1988-1993}) correspond to exact
gate/ingestion failures where unguided \(O(b^d)\) search, non-Bayesian
certainty-factor drift (\texttt{CTA-T1}, \texttt{CTA-T2}), or vanishing
gradients stalled Coherence per Joule.}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Era I (\texttt{1950–1965} · Symbolic \& Threshold Genesis,
  \texttt{AI01}--\texttt{AI06})}: Turing (1950) {[}87{]} inaugurated the
  field by replacing unobservable mentalism with an externalized
  teletype token channel (\texttt{AI01}), though purely conversational
  indistinguishability lacked an internal truth verifier. Two years
  later, Shannon's electromechanical mouse \emph{Theseus}
  (\texttt{1952}, \texttt{AI02}) {[}82{]} demonstrated physical
  stigmergy in silicon-precursor hardware: \texttt{75} telephone relays
  beneath a \texttt{5\ ×\ 5} maze stored \texttt{2}-bit last-exit
  vectors so the mouse externalized trial-and-error wall collisions into
  an \(O(1)\) persistent spatial lookup table. Newell and Simon's
  \emph{Logic Theorist} (\texttt{1956}, \texttt{AI03}) {[}75{]} invented
  dynamic pointer-linked list structures in Information Processing
  Language (\texttt{IPL}) to externalize subgoal proof trees (proving
  \texttt{38} of \texttt{52} \emph{Principia Mathematica} theorems),
  McCarthy (\texttt{1960}, \texttt{AI05}) {[}71{]} unified executable
  code and symbolic data in homoiconic \texttt{Lisp} S-expressions, and
  Robinson (\texttt{1965}, \texttt{AI06}) {[}78{]} compressed
  first-order deduction into a single machine-checkable
  resolution-and-unification gate (deriving the empty clause
  \texttt{{[}{]}}). In parallel, Rosenblatt (\texttt{1958},
  \texttt{AI04}) {[}80{]} introduced the sub-symbolic
  \texttt{Perceptron}, summing weighted photoreceptor signals across a
  hard linear step gate \(\mathbf{w}^\top \mathbf{x} \ge \theta\).
\item
  \textbf{Era II (\texttt{1969–1981} · Rule Bases, Actors, Blackboards
  \& Exact Diagnosis of AI Winter I, \texttt{AI07}--\texttt{AI10})}:
  Minsky and Papert (\texttt{1969}, \texttt{AI07}) {[}73{]} proved
  algebraically that single-layer order-\texttt{1} threshold gates
  cannot represent parity (\texttt{XOR}) or global topological
  connectedness; because no low-cost credit-assignment channel through
  multi-layer step gates was deployed, sub-symbolic research froze.
  Symbolic engineering responded by externalizing human domain
  heuristics into modular \texttt{if-then} production rules
  (\texttt{Heuristic\ DENDRAL} and \texttt{MYCIN}, \texttt{1971–1976},
  \texttt{AI08}) {[}55, 84{]}, asynchronous point-to-point
  \texttt{Actor} mailboxes
  (\texttt{Hewitt,\allowbreak{}\ Bishop,\allowbreak{}\ and\ Steiger\ 1973}, \texttt{AI09})
  {[}64{]}, and the \texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II} multi-specialist
  \texttt{Blackboard} workspace (\texttt{Erman\ et\ al.\allowbreak{}\ 1980},
  \texttt{AI10}) {[}60{]}. \textbf{\texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II} exposes the exact
  mechanistic cause of AI Winter I (\texttt{1974–1980})}: independent
  Knowledge Sources (\texttt{KSs}) communicated cleanly over a shared
  multi-level blackboard, \textbf{but hand-authored crisp rules lacked
  differentiable perceptual ingestion from noisy acoustic waveforms and
  lacked low-cost learned search priors}. Without a sound gradient or
  admissible pruning gate, competing parse hypotheses triggered
  \(O(b^d)\) combinatorial branching while heuristic
  \texttt{MYCIN}-style Certainty Factors (\texttt{CF}) accumulated
  non-Bayesian multi-hop drift (\texttt{CTA-T1}), collapsing
  \texttt{CpJ} as vocabularies and rule counts scaled past \(10^3\).
\item
  \textbf{Era III (\texttt{1982–1998} · Gradient Channels, Graphical
  Message Passing \& Gated Memory, \texttt{AI11}--\texttt{AI17})}:
  Recovery came from inventing continuous and probabilistically sound
  communication channels. Hopfield (\texttt{1982}, \texttt{AI11})
  {[}66{]} cast associative recall as Lyapunov energy relaxation
  (\(\Delta E \le 0\)); Rumelhart, Hinton, and Williams (\texttt{1986},
  \texttt{AI12}) {[}81{]} established reverse-mode backpropagation as a
  backward error-broadcast channel breaking the hidden-layer
  \texttt{XOR} barrier; Pearl (\texttt{1988}, \texttt{AI13}) {[}77{]}
  replaced inconsistent Certainty Factors with
  \texttt{d}-separation-gated belief propagation on Bayesian directed
  acyclic graphs (\texttt{DAGs}); Watkins and Dayan (\texttt{1989/1992},
  \texttt{AI14}) {[}89{]} broadcast delayed environmental rewards
  backward via temporal-difference (\texttt{TD}) \texttt{Q-\allowbreak{}learning};
  LeCun et al.~(\texttt{1989/1998}, \texttt{AI15}) {[}69{]} enforced
  translation-equivariant weight sharing in \texttt{ConvNets}
  (\texttt{LeNet-5}); and Cortes and Vapnik (\texttt{1995},
  \texttt{AI16}) {[}56{]} externalized decision boundaries onto sparse
  Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (\texttt{KKT}) support vectors. Crucially,
  \textbf{AI Winter II (\texttt{1988–1993})} reflected both specialized
  Lisp-hardware economic collapse against commodity microprocessors and
  exponential gradient vanishing across deep saturating sigmoids
  (\texttt{CTA-T2} in backward form). Hochreiter and Schmidhuber
  (\texttt{1997}, \texttt{AI17}) {[}65{]} solved temporal vanishing by
  inventing Long Short-Term Memory (\texttt{LSTM}): a linear Constant
  Error Carousel (\texttt{CEC}) protected by multiplicative input,
  forget, and output gates in \([0,1]\) --- \textbf{the exact
  computational analog of a cyanobacterial \texttt{SepJ} nanopore
  (\texttt{CL03})}, sealing stored gradient state against noisy
  read/write perturbations across \texttt{\textgreater{}1,000} recurrent
  steps.
\item
  \textbf{Era IV (\texttt{2006–2016} · Shared Corpora, Skip Highways,
  Soft Attention \& Self-Play Ratchets, \texttt{AI18}--\texttt{AI23})}:
  Coulom (\texttt{2006}) and Kocsis and Szepesvari (\texttt{2006})
  (\texttt{AI18}) {[}57{]} externalized asymmetric lookahead statistics
  into Upper Confidence Bound for Trees (\texttt{UCT}) Monte Carlo Tree
  Search (\texttt{MCTS}). Deng et al.'s crowd-verified
  \texttt{14M}-image \texttt{ImageNet} ledger (\texttt{2009}) coupled to
  Krizhevsky, Sutskever, and Hinton's dual-\texttt{GPU} \texttt{ReLU}
  \texttt{AlexNet} (\texttt{2012}, \texttt{AI19}) {[}59, 68{]} ignited
  modern scale; Mikolov et al.'s \texttt{Word2Vec} (\texttt{2013}) and
  Mnih et al.'s \texttt{DQN} circular experience replay buffer
  (\texttt{2015}, \texttt{AI20}) {[}72, 74{]} stabilized continuous
  vector geometry and off-policy \texttt{RL}; Goodfellow et al.'s
  Generative Adversarial Networks (\texttt{GANs}, \texttt{2014}) and
  Bahdanau, Cho, and Bengio's content-addressed soft attention
  (\texttt{2015}, \texttt{AI21}) {[}53, 62{]} introduced competitive
  discriminator gates and dynamic alignment; and He et al.'s
  \texttt{ResNet} (\texttt{2016}, \texttt{AI22}) {[}63{]} created an
  unattenuated identity skip highway
  (\(\mathbf{y} = \mathbf{x} + \mathcal{F}(\mathbf{x})\)) across
  \texttt{152+} layers. Culminating Era IV, Silver et al.'s
  \texttt{AlphaGo} and \texttt{AlphaZero} (\texttt{2016–2017},
  \texttt{AI23}) {[}85{]} closed a bidirectional \textbf{\texttt{MCTS}
  \(\leftrightarrow\) Policy/Value self-play distillation ratchet}:
  neural intuition pruned \texttt{MCTS} branching, \texttt{MCTS} search
  externalized improved visit distributions \(\pi_{\text{MCTS}}\), and
  an exact deterministic game-rules verifier
  (\texttt{\textgreater{}=\ 55\%} Elo gate) prevented regression
  (\texttt{CTA-T5}).
\item
  \textbf{Era V (\texttt{2017–2022} · All-to-All Attention, Sparse
  \texttt{MoE}, \texttt{Evoformer} \& Human-Feedback Gates,
  \texttt{AI24}--\texttt{AI28})}: Vaswani et al.'s \texttt{Transformer}
  and Gilmer et al.'s Message Passing Neural Networks (\texttt{MPNN},
  \texttt{2017}, \texttt{AI24}) {[}61, 88{]} replaced sequential
  recurrence with \(O(1)\)-hop random-access Key-Value (\texttt{KV}) and
  molecular graph message passing. Shazeer et al.~(\texttt{2017}) and
  Fedus, Zoph, and Shazeer (\texttt{2022}, \texttt{AI25}, \texttt{CL18})
  {[}21, 83{]} introduced top-\(k\) \texttt{Sparse\ MoE} conditional
  routing (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}3\%–12\%} active weights/token).
  Brown et al.'s \texttt{GPT-3} and Lewis et al.'s Retrieval-Augmented
  Generation (\texttt{RAG}, \texttt{2020}, \texttt{AI26}) {[}54, 70{]}
  paired web-corpus in-context conditioning with non-parametric document
  indices. Jumper et al.'s \texttt{AlphaFold\ 2} (\texttt{2021},
  \texttt{AI27}) {[}67{]} solved monomeric protein folding by coupling
  Multiple Sequence Alignment (\texttt{MSA}) coevolutionary statistics
  to pairwise residue geometry via triangle updates and
  \texttt{SE(3)}-equivariant Invariant Point Attention (\texttt{IPA}).
  Finally, Ouyang et al.'s \texttt{InstructGPT} (\texttt{RLHF}), Wei et
  al.'s Chain-of-Thought (\texttt{CoT}), and Yao et al.'s \texttt{ReAct}
  (\texttt{2022–2023}, \texttt{AI28}) {[}76, 90{]} externalized
  intermediate working memory into natural-language token scratchpads
  and tool-observation loops --- unlocking multi-step synthesis while
  simultaneously exposing Era V's failure mode: \(O(L^2)\) \texttt{KV}
  joule burn, sycophantic human-preference drift, and \texttt{3.71x}
  unattested multi-agent transcript bloat (\texttt{Arm\ C} in
  \texttt{CTA-E2}).
\item
  \textbf{Era VI (\texttt{2023–2025} · Verifier-Gated Search, Latent
  World Models \& Machine Culture, \texttt{AI29}--\texttt{AI34})}:
  Assran, LeCun et al.'s \texttt{I-JEPA} (\texttt{2023}, \texttt{AI29})
  {[}52{]} eliminated wasteful surface-pixel reconstruction by
  predicting masked targets directly in abstract latent space. Brinkmann
  et al.~(\texttt{2023,\allowbreak{}\ 2026}) and Zhuang's \texttt{POLIS}
  (\texttt{2025}, \texttt{AI30}, \texttt{CL19}) {[}6, 7, 50{]} proved
  post-removal cultural lock-in and peer-verified \texttt{LLM} ratchet
  gains. Romera-Paredes et al.'s \texttt{FunSearch} (\texttt{2024},
  \texttt{AI31}) {[}79{]} coupled \texttt{LLM} code mutation to an exact
  sandbox fitness evaluator across evolutionary islands, and Trinh et
  al.'s \texttt{AlphaGeometry} (\texttt{2024}, \texttt{AI32}) {[}86{]}
  paired neural auxiliary point proposals with a sound Deductive
  Database plus Algebraic Rules (\texttt{DD+AR}) engine
  (\texttt{25\ /\ 30} Olympiad geometry problems with zero hallucinated
  proofs). Anthropic's \texttt{Model\ Context\ Protocol} (\texttt{MCP},
  \texttt{2024}, \texttt{AI33}) {[}51{]} standardized typed
  \texttt{JSON-\allowbreak{}RPC\ 2.\allowbreak{}0} tool schemas (\texttt{N\ +\ M}
  vs.~\(N \times M\) adapters), and \texttt{DeepSeek-\allowbreak{}R1} (\texttt{2025},
  \texttt{AI34}) {[}58{]} proved via Group Relative Policy Optimization
  (\texttt{GRPO}) that pure reinforcement learning against deterministic
  math/compiler verifiers spontaneously induces self-verification ---
  yet still re-expends thousands of thinking tokens
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}10–50+\ J}) on recurring structural problem
  classes.
\item
  \textbf{Era VII (\texttt{2026} · Downward Crystallization \& Digital
  Enzymatic Synthesis, \texttt{AI35}--\texttt{AI38})}: Companion Open
  Interface Engineering studies \texttt{{[}same\ house{]}} {[}44{]} and
  this work close the missing downward loop: \texttt{Shape\ D}
  (\texttt{DNULLMM1}, \texttt{AI35}) freezes verified repairs into
  \(O(1)\) \texttt{1.77\ µJ} memory-mapped hash tables; \texttt{AirMoL}
  and \texttt{Downward\ LUT6\ Compilation} (\texttt{AI36}) compile hot
  decision paths into \texttt{81\ ×\ RAMB36E2} single-cycle hardware
  look-up pages (\texttt{0} \texttt{DSP48E2} slices,
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}12\ pJ} \texttt{est\_j}); \texttt{RCE} +
  \texttt{FormEBM} (\texttt{AI37}) pairs a \texttt{47.09\ mJ} latent
  energy pre-filter (\texttt{0.8922} \texttt{AUROC}, \texttt{2.39x}
  fewer checker calls) with a \texttt{\textasciitilde{}49\ mJ} Reality
  Code Engine (\texttt{RCE}) checker (\texttt{1,718x} cheaper than
  frontier model calls); and \texttt{CTA} / \texttt{DES-10}
  (\texttt{AI38}, \texttt{CL20}) unifies all \texttt{10} AI camps behind
  \texttt{\textless{}\ 1\ mJ} \texttt{BLAKE3} + \texttt{Ed25519} Digital
  Enzymes and a provable 4-tier downward compiler (\texttt{CTA-T32} to
  \texttt{CTA-T45}).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 2: Historical Ratchet
Diagnosis (\texttt{1950}--\texttt{2026}) --- Why \texttt{1975}
\texttt{MYCIN} and \texttt{1980} \texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II} Stalled into AI
Winter I While \texttt{2016} \texttt{AlphaGo} and \texttt{2024}
\texttt{FunSearch} Ignited
(\texttt{CTA-T1}--\texttt{CTA-T6})}{Worked Example 2: Historical Ratchet Diagnosis (1950--2026) --- Why 1975 MYCIN and 1980 Hearsay-II Stalled into AI Winter I While 2016 AlphaGo and 2024 FunSearch Ignited (CTA-T1--CTA-T6)}}\label{worked-example-2-historical-ratchet-diagnosis-19502026-why-1975-mycin-and-1980-hearsay-ii-stalled-into-ai-winter-i-while-2016-alphago-and-2024-funsearch-ignited-cta-t1cta-t6}

Mapping historical artificial intelligence architectures onto the
three-phase operator \((\mathcal{E}, \mathcal{G}, \mathcal{T})\)
explains why early multi-module systems stalled while modern
generate-and-verify loops achieved super-human lock-in
(\texttt{{[}E{]}}, \texttt{{[}T{]}}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Why \texttt{MYCIN} (\texttt{1975}) and \texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II}
  (\texttt{1980}) Stalled}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{Externalization (\(\mathcal{E}\))}: \texttt{MYCIN}
    externalized \texttt{\textasciitilde{}450} hand-authored
    \texttt{Lisp} production rules carrying subjective Certainty Factors
    (\texttt{CF}, \(\mathrm{CF} \in [-1, 1]\)); \texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II}
    externalized multi-granular speech hypotheses (acoustic, phonetic,
    syllabic, lexical, phrasal) onto a global shared blackboard polled
    by \(K\) concurrent Knowledge Sources (\texttt{KS}).
  \item
    \emph{Unsound Gate (\(\mathcal{G}\))}: Neither architecture
    possessed an exact truth-preserving admission gate
    (\(V(m) = 1 \not\Rightarrow \text{valid}(m)\)). Because
    \texttt{MYCIN}'s \texttt{CF} combination algebra treated correlated
    clinical evidence as conditionally independent without directed
    acyclic graph (\texttt{DAG}) \texttt{d}-separation, chaining \(k\)
    rules accumulated calibration drift \(\mathrm{err}_0 + k\epsilon\)
    (\texttt{CTA-T1}) and suffered multiplicative fidelity decay \(f^k\)
    (\texttt{CTA-T2}: at per-hop rule precision \(f = 0.92\) across
    \(k = 12\) chained rules, joint validity drops to
    \(0.92^{12} = 0.3677\)). Simultaneously, \texttt{Hearsay-\allowbreak{}II}'s
    unthresholded blackboard forced \(O(K \cdot |B|)\) scheduler checks
    across noisy bottom-up syllable fragments (\texttt{CTA-T9}),
    spending the majority of compute arbitrations on false-positive
    partial parses.
  \item
    \emph{Missing Downward Distillation (\(\mathcal{T}\))}: Extending
    coverage from \texttt{450} to \texttt{4,500} rules required human
    specialists to audit pairwise rule interactions manually, stalling
    into \textbf{AI Winter I} when maintenance energy outgrew diagnostic
    yield.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Why \texttt{AlphaGo} (\texttt{2016}) and \texttt{FunSearch}
  (\texttt{2024}) Ignited}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \emph{Externalization (\(\mathcal{E}\))}: \texttt{AlphaGo}
    externalized learned policy and value priors
    \((p_\theta, v_\theta)\) into an explicit Monte Carlo Tree Search
    (\texttt{MCTS}) state-action table \((N(s,a), Q(s,a))\);
    \texttt{FunSearch} externalized compact Python priority functions
    (\texttt{10} to \texttt{30} lines of code, not natural-language
    essays) into a multi-island evolutionary program database.
  \item
    \emph{Sound Deterministic Gate (\(\mathcal{G}\))}: \texttt{AlphaGo}
    gated legal transitions and terminal outcomes via exact Go board
    rules; \texttt{FunSearch} gated every language-model mutation
    through a deterministic combinatorial witness checker (verifying
    exact cap-set independence or bin-packing capacity in milliseconds).
    Any hallucinated or sub-optimal code received
    \texttt{admittedWeight\ =\ 0} (\texttt{CTA-T3}, \texttt{CTA-T17}),
    keeping island contamination strictly at \texttt{0.0\%} even when
    \textgreater{}\texttt{99\%} of raw model samples were non-improving.
  \item
    \emph{Non-Regressing Lock-In (\(\mathcal{T}\))}: \texttt{AlphaGo}
    distilled expensive multi-thousand-rollout \texttt{MCTS}
    distributions downward into fast single-pass neural weights, while
    \texttt{FunSearch} retained strictly higher-scoring verified
    programs (\(Q(t+1) \ge Q(t)\), \texttt{CTA-T5}, \texttt{CTA-T6})
    across islands --- converting noisy stochastic proposals into a
    permanent mathematical ratchet.
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{\texorpdfstring{5. Event-Driven Attested Orchestration,
Five-Host Fleet Telemetry, and Ten-Camp Synthesis (\texttt{CTA-E1} to
\texttt{CTA-E5})}{5. Event-Driven Attested Orchestration, Five-Host Fleet Telemetry, and Ten-Camp Synthesis (CTA-E1 to CTA-E5)}}\label{event-driven-attested-orchestration-five-host-fleet-telemetry-and-ten-camp-synthesis-cta-e1-to-cta-e5}

Biological coordination across every evolutionary rung surveyed in
Sections 2 and 3 runs on local concentration gradients, cooperative
activation thresholds, and selective pore sealing rather than global
clock ticks or unverified broadcast chatter. Translating those
mechanisms into software engineering yields an event-driven
orchestration architecture in which heterogeneous compute kernels and
language models communicate over energy-budgeted, cryptographically
attested channels. This section specifies the four structural components
of the Communication Tool Avalanche (CTA) orchestrator, states the
pre-registered falsifiers (\texttt{F1} to \texttt{F6}, locked prior to
execution under Secure Hash Algorithm \texttt{SHA-256} digest
\texttt{5739bcdd\allowbreak{}6c356628\allowbreak{}33a470bc\allowbreak{}56bd64f9\allowbreak{}91774c2d\allowbreak{}920a5a9c\allowbreak{}059c0ace\allowbreak{}bdf55e7f}),
and reports executed hardware measurements across three experiments
(\texttt{CTA-E1}, \texttt{CTA-E2}, and \texttt{CTA-E3}) on a 20-thread
\texttt{13th\ Gen\ Intel\ Core\ i9-\allowbreak{}13900H} Linux host
(\texttt{rustc\ 1.\allowbreak{}100.\allowbreak{}0}, \texttt{Python\ 3.\allowbreak{}14.\allowbreak{}4}).

\subsection{5.1 Measurement Grammar and Telemetry
Disclosure}\label{measurement-grammar-and-telemetry-disclosure}

All local processor energy values in \texttt{CTA-E1} through
\texttt{CTA-E3} are read from Linux \texttt{sysfs} \texttt{powercap}
Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) counters using wrap-safe 64-bit
accumulation modulo \texttt{max\_\allowbreak{}energy\_\allowbreak{}range\_\allowbreak{}uj\ =\ 262,\allowbreak{}143,\allowbreak{}328,\allowbreak{}850}
microjoules (\texttt{262.\allowbreak{}143\ J}). Every measurement adheres strictly to
three separation rules:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{On-chip \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} versus external wall power}: No
  external inline shunt or alternating-current (AC) board power analyzer
  was attached (\texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j\ =\ None},
  \texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ false}). All local energy figures are
  stamped \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}.
\item
  \textbf{Three-rail RAPL disclosure (\texttt{package-0}, \texttt{core},
  and \texttt{psys})}: Every experiment records \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0}
  (\texttt{package-0}, central processing unit (CPU) and system-on-chip
  (SoC) socket energy \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}),
  \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0:0} (\texttt{core}, processor core plane
  \texttt{core\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}), and \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:1} (\texttt{psys},
  platform counter \texttt{psys\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}). On this laptop host,
  original equipment manufacturer (OEM) Embedded Controller (EC)
  firmware leaves \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:1} (\texttt{psys}) at a static
  quiescent rate of \texttt{\textasciitilde{}0.202\ W}
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}0.20\ J/s}) that does not scale when
  \texttt{package-0} rises from \texttt{\textasciitilde{}5.0\ W} idle to
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}27.7\ W} under single-core load. Primary
  local Coherence per Joule (CpJ) is therefore computed against dynamic
  \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j} (and reported alongside
  \texttt{core\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}), while \texttt{psys\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j} is
  preserved verbatim in every JSON artifact and ledger line.
\item
  \textbf{Strict separation of local joules from remote cloud tokens}:
  When an arm invokes a remote Application Programming Interface (API)
  large language model (LLM), remote datacenter server energy is
  recorded strictly as \texttt{not\_\allowbreak{}available} and never imputed from
  heuristic constants. Exact API call counts, prompt and completion
  token counts, and United States dollars (USD) at public list rates
  (\texttt{\textbackslash{}\$3.00} per million input tokens and
  \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$15.00} per million output tokens for
  \texttt{Claude\ Sonnet\ 5.\allowbreak{}5}) are reported in dedicated columns
  alongside local \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{5.2 Orchestrator Architecture, Attested Channels, and
Min-Cost
Flow}\label{orchestrator-architecture-attested-channels-and-min-cost-flow}

Let \(G = (V, E)\) denote a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of execution
nodes \(u \in V\) connected by typed directed channels \((i, u) \in E\):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Heterogeneous execution pool and \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json}}:
  Deterministic nodes execute lexical and abstract syntax tree (AST)
  parsing (\texttt{parse\_\allowbreak{}kernel}), scope, mutable-default, and
  type-coercion analysis (\texttt{static\_\allowbreak{}type\_\allowbreak{}scope\_\allowbreak{}kernel}),
  docstring and algebraic invariant synthesis
  (\texttt{test\_\allowbreak{}gen\_\allowbreak{}kernel}), bounded AST/token repair
  (\texttt{det\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}kernel}), and isolated behavioral admission
  (\texttt{diff\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}kernel}). Nondeterministic synthesis
  (\texttt{llm\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}proposer}) sits on a high-cost escalation branch.
  Prior to orchestration runs, every deterministic kernel and
  cryptographic primitive is profiled across three input-size buckets
  (\texttt{\textless{}\ 320\ B}, \texttt{320-\allowbreak{}640\ B},
  \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 640\ B}) into a hardware-keyed lookup table
  (\texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json}).
\item
  \textbf{Constant-time cryptographic channel attestation}: Every
  channel edge carries an envelope signed by the sender's deterministic
  \texttt{Ed25519} keypair (\texttt{64}-byte Request for Comments (RFC)
  \texttt{8032} strict signature via \texttt{ed25519-\allowbreak{}dalek}) over a
  domain-separated header and the \texttt{256}-bit \texttt{BLAKE3}
  digest of the payload bytes. Receiving nodes check sender
  authorization, constant-time \texttt{BLAKE3} digest equality, strict
  \texttt{Ed25519} signature validity, and JavaScript Object Notation
  (JSON) frame completeness before mutating node state.
\item
  \textbf{Successive Shortest Path (SSP) min-cost flow scheduler}: Given
  edge capacities \(c(i, u)\) and microjoule-scaled weights \(w(i, u)\)
  drawn from \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json}, the compiled Rust scheduler
  computes a minimum-cost flow of demand \(D\) from source \(s\) to
  commit sink \(t\):
\end{enumerate}

\[
\min_{f} \sum_{(i, u) \in E} w(i, u)\, f(i, u) \quad \text{subject to} \quad 0 \le f(i, u) \le c(i, u), \quad \sum_{i} f(i, u) - \sum_{v} f(u, v) = b(u),
\]

using Bellman-Ford initial node potentials \(\pi(u)\) followed by
Johnson reduced-cost Dijkstra augmentations
\(w_{\pi}(i, u) = w(i, u) + \pi(i) - \pi(u) \ge 0\). Because the
schedule depends only on graph topology and \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json},
\texttt{SSP} executes once at topology epoch \texttt{0}
(\texttt{2.11\ µs} for the \texttt{CTA-E2} pool) and recomputes solely
when a node drops out or joins (\texttt{0} per-tick scheduler overhead).

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{3}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Thresholded integrate-and-fire wakeup (zero clocks, zero
  polling)}: Each node \(u\) maintains an accumulated membrane potential
  \(V_u\) over incoming weighted signals \(s_i\):
\end{enumerate}

\[
V_u(t) = \sum_{(i, u) \in E} w_{iu}\, s_i(t), \qquad \text{fire}(u) \iff V_u(t) \ge \theta_u.
\]

Worker threads block in the Linux kernel via \texttt{eventfd}
(\texttt{EFD\_\allowbreak{}CLOEXEC}), consuming zero CPU cycles while
\(V_u < \theta_u\). Upstream node \(i\) writes to \texttt{eventfd}
strictly at the threshold-crossing transition
\(V_u < \theta_u \to V_u \ge \theta_u\). Every firing appends an
immutable receipt
\texttt{(\allowbreak{}trace\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}\ step\_\allowbreak{}idx,\allowbreak{}\ node\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}\ input\_\allowbreak{}blake3,\allowbreak{}\ output\_\allowbreak{}blake3,\allowbreak{}\ ed25519\_\allowbreak{}sig\_\allowbreak{}prefix,\allowbreak{}\ verify\_\allowbreak{}ns,\allowbreak{}\ psys\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j,\allowbreak{}\ pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j,\allowbreak{}\ latency\_\allowbreak{}ms,\allowbreak{}\ potential\_\allowbreak{}v,\allowbreak{}\ threshold\_\allowbreak{}theta)}
to the energy ledger.

Across a \texttt{1,024x} increase in channel payload size
(\texttt{64\ B} to \texttt{64\ KiB}), compiled Rust \texttt{BLAKE3} +
\texttt{Ed25519} verification latency rises by \texttt{1.44x}
(\texttt{25.95\ µs} to \texttt{37.39\ µs} median) and socket energy
stays under \texttt{1\ mJ} (\texttt{643.\allowbreak{}03\ µJ} to \texttt{926.\allowbreak{}42\ µJ}),
satisfying pre-registered \texttt{Falsifier\ 2}
(\texttt{\textless{}\ 100\ µs} and \texttt{\textless{}\ 5\ mJ} per
edge).

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{5.3 \texttt{CTA-E1}: Event-Driven
Thresholded Gradients vs.~Clocked Polling
Tax}{5.3 CTA-E1: Event-Driven Thresholded Gradients vs.~Clocked Polling Tax}}\label{cta-e1-event-driven-thresholded-gradients-vs.-clocked-polling-tax}

\texttt{CTA-E1} instantiates a 5-layer, 16-node attested orchestration
DAG (\texttt{16} worker OS threads exchanging \texttt{BLAKE3} +
\texttt{Ed25519} envelopes where internal layers integrate \texttt{2} to
\texttt{4} upstream inputs before crossing \(\theta_u = 1.0\)) driven by
bursty micro-batch arrivals across \texttt{1\%}, \texttt{5\%}, and
\texttt{20\%} target active duty cycles (\texttt{10.0\ s} window per
run, \texttt{4} scheduling regimes interleaved within each of \texttt{5}
replicates = \texttt{60} windows, \texttt{600\ s} total runtime).
Thread-wide OS context switches are summed across
\texttt{/\allowbreak{}proc/\allowbreak{}self/\allowbreak{}task/\allowbreak{}*/\allowbreak{}status}.

\textbf{\texttt{Falsifier\ 6} baseline-drift disclosure on
\texttt{CTA-E1}}: Quiet \texttt{5.0\ s} pre-benchmark idle power
measured \texttt{3.95\ W} (\texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j\ =\ 19.\allowbreak{}77\ J}),
whereas the immediate \texttt{5.0\ s} post-benchmark tail right after
\texttt{600\ s} of load measured \texttt{20.13\ W} (\texttt{410.2\%}
thermal/background tail drift, exceeding the \texttt{10.0\%}
\texttt{Falsifier\ 6} gate; a transient thermal/background plateau also
elevated raw \texttt{5\%}-duty replicates relative to \texttt{20\%}-duty
replicates). Per pre-registered \texttt{Falsifier\ 6}, derived
idle-subtracted net joules are \textbf{withheld} for \texttt{CTA-E1},
and only raw unsubtracted \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} values and
within-replicate interleaved comparisons are reported.

\textbf{\texttt{2.76} / \texttt{55.19}} \\
\texttt{20\%} (\texttt{1,020} events/win) & \texttt{10\ Hz} Clocked Poll
& \texttt{231.45\ J} & \texttt{186.90\ J} & \texttt{2.37\ J} &
\texttt{1,582.0} (\texttt{81.0}) & \texttt{2,046.2} & \texttt{547.62} /
\texttt{721.74} \\
\texttt{20\%} (\texttt{1,020} events/win) & \texttt{100\ Hz} Clocked
Poll & \texttt{234.04\ J} & \texttt{188.89\ J} & \texttt{2.35\ J} &
\texttt{15,649.6} (\texttt{9,280.2}) & \texttt{16,117.0} &
\texttt{52.07} / \texttt{99.54} \\
\texttt{20\%} (\texttt{1,020} events/win) & \texttt{1,000\ Hz} Clocked
Poll & \texttt{269.13\ J} & \texttt{223.87\ J} & \texttt{2.32\ J} &
\texttt{148,023.6} (\texttt{142,013.6}) & \texttt{148,638.2} &
\texttt{7.43} / \texttt{59.39} \\
\end{longtable}
}

Clocked polling faces a structural latency-versus-joules dilemma across
multi-hop graphs (\texttt{Falsifier\ 1}). Slowing the clock to
\texttt{10\ Hz} keeps wakeup energy low (\texttt{1,600} wakeups per
\texttt{10\ s}), but multi-stage tick alignment inflates median
event-to-commit latency by \texttt{200x} to \texttt{221x}
(\texttt{547.\allowbreak{}62-\allowbreak{}570.\allowbreak{}38\ ms} versus \texttt{2.\allowbreak{}58-\allowbreak{}2.\allowbreak{}76\ ms}). Accelerating
the clock to \texttt{1,000\ Hz} narrows median latency to
\texttt{7.\allowbreak{}34-\allowbreak{}7.\allowbreak{}46\ ms} (still \texttt{2.7x} slower than direct
\texttt{eventfd} handoff), yet triggers
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}150,000} thread wakeups per \texttt{10\ s}
window (\texttt{99.8\%} empty at \texttt{1\%} duty), preventing
processor C-state residency and burning \texttt{+164.\allowbreak{}40\ J}
(\texttt{2.21x} socket energy, \texttt{2.69x} core energy) per
\texttt{10\ s} window at \texttt{1\%} duty (\texttt{5\ /\ 5} paired
replicates positive: \texttt{+82.32\ J} to \texttt{+313.\allowbreak{}29\ J}) and
\texttt{+87.97\ J} (\texttt{+27.6\%}) at \texttt{5\%} duty
(\texttt{5\ /\ 5} paired replicates positive: \texttt{+60.87\ J} to
\texttt{+140.\allowbreak{}18\ J}). Zero-clock thresholded \texttt{eventfd} firing
achieves \texttt{0} empty wakeups, lowest \texttt{1\%}-duty energy, and
\texttt{2.\allowbreak{}58-\allowbreak{}2.\allowbreak{}76\ ms} median commit latency simultaneously.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{5.4 \texttt{CTA-E2}: 60-Task Multi-Category
Code-Repair Benchmark (\texttt{240}
Episodes)}{5.4 CTA-E2: 60-Task Multi-Category Code-Repair Benchmark (240 Episodes)}}\label{cta-e2-60-task-multi-category-code-repair-benchmark-240-episodes}

\texttt{CTA-E2} evaluates a frozen \texttt{60}-task Python code-repair
corpus
(\texttt{corpus\_\allowbreak{}sha256\ =\ b4ea0228\allowbreak{}270b8e6a\allowbreak{}e06f935f\allowbreak{}e8b92ed4\allowbreak{}5cf2da4d\allowbreak{}9aa0d486\allowbreak{}8d0a42d4\allowbreak{}e6e9e802})
stratified into three \texttt{20}-task categories:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Category 1 (\texttt{C1\_01}-\texttt{C1\_20})}: Syntax,
  indentation, bracket-delimiter, C-operator, and missing
  standard-library import defects.
\item
  \textbf{Category 2 (\texttt{C2\_01}-\texttt{C2\_20})}: Unbound
  identifier typos, mutable default argument state leaks
  (\texttt{={[}{]}}, \texttt{=\{\}}, \texttt{=set()}), in-place mutator
  returns (\texttt{return\ xs.\allowbreak{}sort(\allowbreak{})}), float-to-int division coercions,
  and container API defects.
\item
  \textbf{Category 3 (\texttt{C3\_01}-\texttt{C3\_20})}: Algorithmic,
  numerical, and state-machine defects (including Tarjan strongly
  connected component back-edge updates, unrestricted
  Damerau-Levenshtein transpositions, lexicographic min-hop Dijkstra,
  overlapping Knuth-Morris-Pratt borders, Neumaier compensated
  summation, dual-Fenwick range updates, banker's rounding
  \texttt{Q16.16} saturation, non-coprime Chinese Remainder Theorem,
  fractional token-bucket cap clamping, reflected \texttt{CRC-\allowbreak{}8/\allowbreak{}MAXIM},
  target-specific negative-cycle Bellman-Ford, escaped run-length
  chunking, and Raft Figure 2 \texttt{AppendEntries} prefix-safe
  conflict truncation).
\end{itemize}

Every task is scored post-commit against an isolated held-out oracle
(\texttt{\textgreater{}=\ 8} unit vectors executed twice on the same
function object to catch state leaks, \texttt{0} oracle access during
repair; \texttt{Falsifier\ 3} satisfied). Pre- versus post-benchmark
\texttt{5.0\ s} quiet idle socket power measured \texttt{6.10\ W} versus
\texttt{5.49\ W} (\texttt{9.93\%} drift, passing \texttt{Falsifier\ 6}
\texttt{\textless{}=\ 10.0\%}). Let verified local Coherence per Joule
be defined as:

\[
\mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{pkg}} = \frac{N_{\text{verified\_correct}}}{E_{\text{pkg\_reported\_j}}}, \qquad \mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{core}} = \frac{N_{\text{verified\_correct}}}{E_{\text{core\_reported\_j}}}.
\]

Three empirical findings stand out (\texttt{Falsifier\ 4}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Deterministic pre-filtering closes \texttt{66.67\%}
  (\texttt{40\ /\ 60}) of defects at millijoule scale}: \texttt{Arm\ A}
  solves \texttt{20\ /\ 20} Category-1 and \texttt{20\ /\ 20} Category-2
  tasks in \texttt{57\ ms} and \texttt{1.51\ J} (\texttt{37.7\ mJ} per
  verified repair, \texttt{0} model calls) while refusing all
  \texttt{20} Category-3 logic defects (\texttt{0} false commits).
  Calling a monolithic LLM (\texttt{Arm\ B}) or a 4-agent chain
  (\texttt{Arm\ C}) on those same \texttt{40} structural tasks spends
  \texttt{395.45\ J} (\texttt{40} calls,
  \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$0.17}) and \texttt{3,\allowbreak{}909.\allowbreak{}60\ J}
  (\texttt{160} calls, \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$1.13}), respectively,
  for zero accuracy gain.
\item
  \textbf{Unattested multi-agent conversation triggers a real
  communication avalanche (\texttt{86.67\%} pass, \texttt{13.33\%} false
  commits)}: In \texttt{Arm\ C}
  (\texttt{Planner\ -\textgreater{}\ Coder\ -\textgreater{}\ Reviewer\ -\textgreater{}\ Summarizer}),
  unverified natural-language transcript passing amplifies mean prompt
  context \texttt{3.71x} across hops
  (\texttt{330.6\ -\textgreater{}\ 600.0\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,011.2\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,225.1}
  input tokens per hop, \texttt{288,583} total tokens). On \texttt{8} of
  the \texttt{20} Category-3 algorithmic tasks (\texttt{C3\_02},
  \texttt{C3\_03}, \texttt{C3\_06}, \texttt{C3\_11}, \texttt{C3\_13},
  \texttt{C3\_15}, \texttt{C3\_17}, \texttt{C3\_18}), cumulative
  conversational commentary and adaptive reasoning exhausted the
  \texttt{Summarizer}'s \texttt{1,000}-token output window mid-function,
  emitting truncated Python blocks that were committed unverified
  (\texttt{40.0\%} Category-3 false-commit rate). Even single-shot
  \texttt{Arm\ B} suffered \texttt{1} truncated false commit
  (\texttt{C3\_05}).
\item
  \textbf{Thresholded escalation + attested commit gating
  (\texttt{Arm\ D}) dominates on safety, cost, and \texttt{CpJ}}:
  \texttt{Arm\ D} keeps \texttt{llm\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}proposer} asleep
  (\(V_{\text{esc}} = 0 < \theta_{\text{esc}} = 1.0\)) on all
  \texttt{40} Category-1/2 tasks (\texttt{1.50\ J} including
  \texttt{322} total Rust \texttt{BLAKE3} + \texttt{Ed25519} ledger
  receipts across the \texttt{60} traces), wakes
  \texttt{Claude\ Sonnet\ 5.\allowbreak{}5} strictly on the \texttt{20} Category-3
  tasks (\texttt{21} total calls), repairs \texttt{C3\_05}
  (\texttt{59\ /\allowbreak{}\ 60\ =\ 98.\allowbreak{}33\%} verified pass rate), and cleanly
  refuses \texttt{C3\_17} when two bounded proposals fail
  \texttt{diff\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}kernel} (\texttt{0\ /\allowbreak{}\ 60\ =\ 0.\allowbreak{}00\%} false
  commits). Relative to \texttt{Arm\ B}, \texttt{Arm\ D} cuts LLM calls
  by \texttt{2.86x}, API USD by \texttt{1.63x}, and local socket joules
  by \texttt{2.09x} (\texttt{CpJ\_pkg} \texttt{0.136}
  vs.~\texttt{0.065}). Relative to the unattested 4-agent chain
  (\texttt{Arm\ C}), \texttt{Arm\ D} raises pass rate by \texttt{+11.67}
  percentage points, eliminates \texttt{8\ /\ 8} false commits, cuts
  tokens by \texttt{10.14x}, cuts API USD by \texttt{7.95x}, cuts wall
  latency by \texttt{12.92x}, and increases local socket Coherence per
  Joule by \texttt{14.97x} (\texttt{15.98x} on
  \texttt{core\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 3: Step-by-Step Hardware
Receipt Walk-Through on \texttt{CTA-E2} Task \texttt{C3\_20}
(\texttt{raft\_\allowbreak{}append\_\allowbreak{}entries})}{Worked Example 3: Step-by-Step Hardware Receipt Walk-Through on CTA-E2 Task C3\_20 (raft\_append\_entries)}}\label{worked-example-3-step-by-step-hardware-receipt-walk-through-on-cta-e2-task-c3_20-raft_append_entries}

To contrast an unattested multi-agent chat cascade (\texttt{Arm\ C})
against thresholded, cryptographically attested orchestration
(\texttt{Arm\ D}) on real hardware
(\texttt{13th\ Gen\ Intel\ Core\ i9-\allowbreak{}13900H}, Linux \texttt{RAPL}
\texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}, \texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ false}), inspect the
exact execution receipts recorded for Category-3 Task \texttt{C3\_20}
(\texttt{raft\_\allowbreak{}append\_\allowbreak{}entries}, implementing prefix-safe log conflict
truncation from Raft \texttt{§5.3}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{The Defect}: The broken implementation unconditionally slices
  \texttt{out\ =\ out{[}:prev\_log\_index{]}\ +\ list(entries)},
  silently deleting already-committed suffix entries
  \texttt{(2,\ \textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{})} whenever a stale,
  shorter matching heartbeat prefix
  \texttt{{[}(1,\ \textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}){]}} arrives at
  \texttt{prev\_\allowbreak{}log\_\allowbreak{}index\ =\ 1}.
\item
  \textbf{Arm A (\texttt{Det-Only} Pool --- Safe Millijoule
  Abstention)}: Deterministic kernels parse and test \texttt{C3\_20} in
  \texttt{1.34\ ms} (\texttt{19.59\ mJ} \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j},
  \texttt{0} model calls), detect that none of the \texttt{16} bounded
  AST/token templates repairs the multi-line loop defect, and
  \textbf{cleanly refuse commit} (\texttt{false\_\allowbreak{}commit\ =\ false}).
\item
  \textbf{Arm C (\texttt{Unattested-\allowbreak{}4-\allowbreak{}Agent} Chat --- Transcript
  Avalanche)}: Passing unverified natural-language commentary across
  \texttt{Planner\ -\textgreater{}\ Coder\ -\textgreater{}\ Reviewer\ -\textgreater{}\ Summarizer}
  (\texttt{4} sequential \texttt{Claude\ Sonnet\ 5.\allowbreak{}5} calls) inflates
  prompt tokens nearly threefold on \texttt{C3\_20}
  (\texttt{581\ -\textgreater{}\ 884\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,642\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,692}
  input tokens; \texttt{4,799} input + \texttt{2,213} output =
  \texttt{7,012} total tokens, \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$0.0476},
  \texttt{67.70\ J} \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}, \texttt{17,\allowbreak{}659.\allowbreak{}76\ ms}
  wall time) and \texttt{3.71x} across the \texttt{60}-task suite
  (\texttt{330.6\ -\textgreater{}\ 600.0\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,011.2\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,225.1}
  mean input tokens, \texttt{5,\allowbreak{}710.\allowbreak{}77\ J} total). Worse, on \texttt{8}
  sibling Category-3 tasks --- such as \texttt{C3\_18}
  (\texttt{502\ -\textgreater{}\ 802\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,343\ -\textgreater{}\ 1,357}
  input tokens, \texttt{71.60\ J}), conversational verbosity overflows
  the \texttt{Summarizer}'s \texttt{1,000}-token output window
  mid-function and commits a raw \texttt{SyntaxError} (\texttt{13.33\%}
  suite false-commit rate).
\item
  \textbf{Arm D (\texttt{CTA-\allowbreak{}Orchestrator} --- Attested \texttt{eventfd}
  Escalation \& Commit Gate)}: Every edge verifies a \texttt{256}-bit
  \texttt{BLAKE3} digest + \texttt{64}-byte \texttt{Ed25519} signature
  (\texttt{27.04\ µs} \texttt{p50} at \texttt{1\ KiB} in
  microbenchmarks) and logs six immutable receipts in
  \texttt{cta\_\allowbreak{}e2\_\allowbreak{}firing\_\allowbreak{}ledger.\allowbreak{}jsonl}
  (\texttt{trace\_\allowbreak{}id\ =\ cta\_\allowbreak{}e2\_\allowbreak{}C3\_\allowbreak{}20}):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{0} (\texttt{parse\_\allowbreak{}kernel})}: Verifies input
    \texttt{7ae1414e…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ 993161b1…}) in
    \texttt{29.38\ µs}; emits AST diagnostic \texttt{9f868fad…} in
    \texttt{0.210\ ms}.
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{1} (\texttt{static\_\allowbreak{}type\_\allowbreak{}scope\_\allowbreak{}kernel})}:
    Verifies \texttt{9f868fad…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ 48e2700c…}) in
    \texttt{25.93\ µs}; confirms clean lexical/type scope
    \texttt{3d82a573…} in \texttt{0.336\ ms}.
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{2} (\texttt{test\_\allowbreak{}gen\_\allowbreak{}kernel})}: Verifies
    \texttt{3d82a573…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ 6d264e0c…}) in
    \texttt{26.19\ µs}; extracts public doctest invariant
    \texttt{92bf4238…} in \texttt{0.068\ ms}.
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{3} (\texttt{det\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}kernel})}: Verifies
    \texttt{92bf4238…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ 8b10b040…}) in
    \texttt{31.08\ µs}; tests bounded AST repairs in \texttt{0.755\ ms}
    (\texttt{18.62\ mJ}), sees doctest failure on stale-prefix
    preservation, and writes \texttt{1} across Linux \texttt{eventfd} so
    \(V_{\text{esc}} = 1.0 \ge \theta_{\text{esc}} = 1.0\).
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{4} (\texttt{llm\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}proposer})}: Wakes from
    zero-clock kernel sleep for \textbf{one} structured call
    (\texttt{738} input + \texttt{569} output = \texttt{1,307} tokens,
    \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$0.0107}), verifies envelope
    \texttt{c2bfa1ba…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ b1d75063…}) in
    \texttt{50.12\ µs}, and emits index-walking conflict check
    \texttt{ad1fc058…} (\texttt{2,\allowbreak{}957.\allowbreak{}59\ ms}, \texttt{17.56\ J}
    \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}).
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{5} (\texttt{diff\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}kernel})}: Verifies
    \texttt{f138e9ca…} (\texttt{sig\ =\ 7ffe7c3f…}) in
    \texttt{59.48\ µs}, executes isolated doctest + repeat-call
    state-leak check in \texttt{1.009\ ms} (\texttt{14.16\ mJ}), and
    commits \texttt{62ccce23…} (\texttt{17.65\ J} episode total ---
    \texttt{3.84x} lower socket joules, \texttt{5.36x} fewer tokens, and
    \texttt{5.96x} faster than \texttt{Arm\ C} on \texttt{C3\_20}, with
    \textbf{\texttt{0\ /\ 60} false commits} across the entire
    benchmark).
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{5.5 \texttt{CTA-E3}: Byzantine Channel
Injection and \texttt{SSP} Topology
Failover}{5.5 CTA-E3: Byzantine Channel Injection and SSP Topology Failover}}\label{cta-e3-byzantine-channel-injection-and-ssp-topology-failover}

\texttt{CTA-E3} stress-tests channel integrity and dynamic graph
survivability by injecting \texttt{200} corrupted channel events across
four \texttt{50}-event fault classes alongside \texttt{200} valid
control envelopes (\texttt{200\ /\allowbreak{}\ 200} admitted,
\texttt{p50\ =\ 27.\allowbreak{}51\ µs}), followed by \texttt{20} mid-stream single-
to quad-node dropouts across a \texttt{14}-node primary-plus-standby
orchestration topology (\texttt{Falsifier\ 5}).

{\footnotesize\setlength{\tabcolsep}{3.8pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.15}
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{(\linewidth - 8\tabcolsep) * \real{0.2281}}
