Just as cyanobacterial \texttt{SepJ} nanopores seal locally under
calcium stress to prevent filament-wide lysis, layered channel
attestation + behavioral gating contains \texttt{200\ /\allowbreak{}\ 200}
(\texttt{100.0\%}) injected faults (\texttt{0.00\%} corrupt commits
versus \texttt{84.50\%} downstream propagation in an unattested
regex-extractive chain). Across all \texttt{20} mid-stream node-dropout
scenarios (\texttt{1} to \texttt{4} simultaneous primary
kernel/proposer/verifier failures at flow demand \texttt{D\ =\ 8}),
exact \texttt{SSP} topology recomputation reroutes flow onto standby
paths in \texttt{2.40\ µs} median (\texttt{mean\ =\ 2.\allowbreak{}41\ µs},
\texttt{p99\ =\ 3.\allowbreak{}58\ µs}, \texttt{max\ =\ 3.\allowbreak{}58\ µs}), satisfying
\texttt{20\ /\ 20} (\texttt{100\%}) flow demands with zero dropped
traces.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{5.6 Communication Tool Avalanche
(\texttt{CTA}) Experiment \texttt{CTA-E4}: Five-Host Cross-Silicon and
Cross-Kernel Fleet Energy
Audit}{5.6 Communication Tool Avalanche (CTA) Experiment CTA-E4: Five-Host Cross-Silicon and Cross-Kernel Fleet Energy Audit}}\label{communication-tool-avalanche-cta-experiment-cta-e4-five-host-cross-silicon-and-cross-kernel-fleet-energy-audit}

Single-host benchmarks leave open whether zero-clock thresholded
orchestration and constant-time cryptographic channel attestation remain
sub-millijoule and wakeup-free across heterogeneous instruction-set
architectures and operating system (OS) kernels. Experiment
\texttt{CTA-E4} (locked prior to execution under Secure Hash Algorithm
256-bit (\texttt{SHA-256}) pre-registration digest
\texttt{3efe6840\allowbreak{}7530d203\allowbreak{}d7e8a6a1\allowbreak{}f13d9943\allowbreak{}713f763f\allowbreak{}e2403430\allowbreak{}1f85e533\allowbreak{}0bb2a65b})
compiles a unified cross-platform Rust engine
(\texttt{cta\_\allowbreak{}fleet\_\allowbreak{}rust}, \texttt{rustc\ 1.\allowbreak{}100.\allowbreak{}0}) across five
hardware and kernel targets spanning physical board-level direct-current
(DC) shunts (\texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}, \texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ true}) and
four on-chip or driver telemetry substrates (\texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j},
\texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ false}), organized across whole-board input
(\texttt{(L1)}), rail or socket plane (\texttt{(L2)}), and per-process
(\texttt{(L3)}) telemetry scopes:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{NVIDIA (accelerated-computing system) Jetson AGX
  (Autonomous-Grade Compute) Thor (\texttt{aarch64} Tegra Linux
  \texttt{6.8}, central processing unit (CPU)-only execution)}:
  Respecting an active graphics processing unit (GPU) co-tenant lock
  (\texttt{CUDA\_\allowbreak{}VISIBLE\_\allowbreak{}DEVICES=""}, Compute Unified Device
  Architecture (CUDA) disabled), \texttt{cta\_\allowbreak{}fleet\_\allowbreak{}rust} samples
  physical Texas Instruments (TI) shunt resistors at \texttt{200\ Hz}
  via \texttt{sysfs} \texttt{hwmon}: \texttt{INA238} (whole-board shunt
  monitor) voltage-input rail \texttt{VIN} \texttt{(L1)}
  (\texttt{l1\_\allowbreak{}vin\_\allowbreak{}ina238\_\allowbreak{}measured\_\allowbreak{}j}) and \texttt{INA3221}
  (three-channel rail monitor) channel-2 supply rail
  \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} (voltage drain-drain for CPU,
  system-on-chip (SoC), and memory subsystem (MSS)) \texttt{(L2)}
  (\texttt{l2\_\allowbreak{}vdd\_\allowbreak{}cpu\_\allowbreak{}soc\_\allowbreak{}mss\_\allowbreak{}measured\_\allowbreak{}j}, physically isolated
  from channel-1 \texttt{VDD\_GPU}).
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{13th\ Gen\ Intel\ Core\ i9-\allowbreak{}13900H} (\texttt{20}-thread
  Raptor Lake \texttt{x86\_64} Linux \texttt{7.0})}: Reads wrap-safe
  \texttt{powercap} Running Average Power Limit (RAPL)
  \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0} (\texttt{package-0}) and \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0:0}
  (\texttt{core}) \texttt{(L2)} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}.
\item
  \textbf{Advanced Micro Devices (AMD) Ryzen Artificial Intelligence
  (AI) Max+ \texttt{395} (\texttt{32}-thread Zen 5 Strix Halo +
  \texttt{Radeon\ 8060S} \texttt{x86\_64} Linux \texttt{7.0})}: Reads
  \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0:0} (\texttt{core}), \texttt{intel-\allowbreak{}rapl:0}
  accelerated processing unit (APU) socket, and \texttt{amdgpu} Package
  Power Tracking (PPT) \texttt{(L2)} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Apple\ M3\ Ultra} (\texttt{arm64} macOS Darwin X is
  Not Unix (XNU) \texttt{27.2.0} under \texttt{memguard})}: Reads
  \texttt{proc\_\allowbreak{}pid\_\allowbreak{}rusage(\allowbreak{}RUSAGE\_\allowbreak{}INFO\_\allowbreak{}V6).\allowbreak{}ri\_\allowbreak{}energy\_\allowbreak{}nj} (Resource
  Usage Information Version 6 (\texttt{RUSAGE\_\allowbreak{}INFO\_\allowbreak{}V6})) \texttt{(L3)}
  per-process identifier (PID) \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} with \texttt{kqueue}
  + Portable Operating System Interface (POSIX) \texttt{pipe} kernel
  event blocking.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Windows\ 11\ Pro\ x86-\allowbreak{}64} Bare-Metal + GeForce Ray
  Tracing Texel eXtreme (RTX) \texttt{2060} (New Technology (NT) kernel
  \texttt{10.\allowbreak{}0.\allowbreak{}26300})}: Uses native Win32 \texttt{CreateEventW} /
  \texttt{WaitForSingleObject} blocking versus \texttt{1,000\ Hz}
  high-resolution waitable timers (\texttt{CreateWaitableTimerExW}),
  recording rootless NVIDIA Management Library (NVML) \texttt{nvml.dll}
  cumulative millijoules \texttt{(L2)} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} alongside
  \texttt{QueryProcessCycleTime} \texttt{(L3)} CPU cycles (rootless CPU
  RAPL is not exposed by the Windows NT kernel).
\end{enumerate}

Every measurement preserves strict separation between physical shunt
\texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} and counter-based \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}; values from
distinct evidence classes are never summed. Per pre-registered
\texttt{Falsifier\ 6}, whenever a shared-host co-tenant is active
(\texttt{Jetson\ AGX\ Thor} GPU co-tenant at
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}55\ W} on \texttt{VIN};
\texttt{AMD\ Ryzen\ AI\ Max+\ 395} \texttt{\textasciitilde{}120\ W} APU
co-tenant) or \texttt{5.0\ s} pre/post idle baselines drift above
\texttt{10.0\%}, single-window idle subtraction is withheld in favor of
raw unsubtracted readings and counterbalanced alternating-order
within-pair \texttt{(\allowbreak{}poll\_\allowbreak{}1000hz\ -\allowbreak{}\ event\_\allowbreak{}threshold)} deltas across
four interleaved \texttt{5.0\ s} windows at \texttt{1\%} bursty duty
(\texttt{16}-node directed acyclic graph (DAG)).

Across all five silicon architectures and all three OS kernels (Linux
\texttt{eventfd}, macOS XNU \texttt{kqueue} + \texttt{pipe}, and Windows
NT \texttt{Event}), \texttt{BLAKE3} (\texttt{256}-bit) +
\texttt{Ed25519} (\texttt{64}-byte Request for Comments (RFC)
\texttt{8032}) verification completes in \texttt{23.\allowbreak{}84–50.\allowbreak{}13\ µs} median
at \texttt{1\ KiB} and \texttt{26.\allowbreak{}16–62.\allowbreak{}24\ µs} at \texttt{16\ KiB}
while consuming \texttt{\textless{}\ 1\ mJ} on isolated processor rails
(\texttt{90.81\ µJ} on \texttt{M3\ Ultra} \texttt{L3},
\texttt{201.\allowbreak{}27\ µJ} on \texttt{Zen\ 5} \texttt{core},
\texttt{642.\allowbreak{}38\ µJ} on \texttt{i9-13900H} \texttt{pkg}, and
\textbf{\texttt{927.\allowbreak{}30\ µJ} physical shunt \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} on
\texttt{Jetson\ AGX\ Thor} \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS}}), satisfying
\texttt{Falsifier\ 2} (\texttt{\textless{}\ 100\ µs} and
\texttt{\textless{}\ 5\ mJ} per edge) on every host. Zero-clock
thresholded wakeup eliminates \texttt{100\%} of empty polling wakeups
(\texttt{0.0} versus \texttt{53,\allowbreak{}864–75,\allowbreak{}307} empty wakeups per
\texttt{5.0\ s} window), cuts median commit latency by \texttt{1.90x} to
\texttt{4.29x} across all five hosts, and measurably drops physical
\texttt{INA3221} \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} power and energy on
\texttt{Jetson\ AGX\ Thor}
(\texttt{13.01\ W\ -\textgreater{}\ 12.74\ W}, saving \texttt{+1.34\ J}
\texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} and \texttt{+6.69\ J} \texttt{INA238}
\texttt{VIN} per \texttt{5.0\ s} window even under \texttt{1\%} bursty
duty alongside a shared-host co-tenant) as well as \texttt{3.22x} socket
\texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} (\texttt{9.97x} core) on \texttt{i9-13900H} and
\texttt{6.22x} per-process \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} on \texttt{M3\ Ultra}
(\texttt{Falsifier\ 1} supported).

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{5.7 \texttt{CTA-E5}: Ten Siloed AI Paradigm
Camps versus Immediate Digital Enzymatic Synthesis
(\texttt{DES-10})}{5.7 CTA-E5: Ten Siloed AI Paradigm Camps versus Immediate Digital Enzymatic Synthesis (DES-10)}}\label{cta-e5-ten-siloed-ai-paradigm-camps-versus-immediate-digital-enzymatic-synthesis-des-10}

Contemporary AI research remains partitioned into competing
single-mechanism camps---autoregressive large language model (LLM)
decoding, symbolic Boolean satisfiability (SAT) and satisfiability
modulo theories (SMT) / finite-domain (FD) solvers, latent energy-based
model (EBM) compatibility scorers, Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)
planners using Upper Confidence Bound 1 (\texttt{UCB1}), episodic case
stores, evolutionary abstract syntax tree (AST) mutators, structural
causal \texttt{do}-calculus engines, compiled lookup-table (LUT) and
finite-state machine (FSM) automata, natural-language (NL) multi-agent
debate societies, and embodied black-box sandbox hill-climbers---each
treating its local representation as sufficient for general agency. In
biological evolution, multicellular complexity did not wait for one cell
type to absorb every metabolic function; secreted signaling molecules,
selective gap junctions, and catalytic enzymes lowered inter-organ
activation barriers so distinct tissues could specialize and coordinate
immediately.

Experiment \texttt{CTA-E5} tests whether \texttt{CTA} orchestration
enables immediate \texttt{DES-10} multi-organ synthesis across all ten
paradigms. Using canonical binary intermediate representation (IR)
frames, a Digital Enzyme bridging upstream camp \(a\) to downstream camp
\(b\) is defined as a four-tuple:

\[
\mathcal{E}_{a \to b} = \bigl(\Phi_{\mathrm{IR}},\, \mathrm{BLAKE3} + \mathrm{Ed25519},\, V_{\mathrm{gate}},\, \theta_b\bigr),
\]

where \(\Phi_{\mathrm{IR}}\) packs canonical zero-copy binary IR frames
(\texttt{DE10} header plus typed payload bytes, eliminating NL prose
serialization and re-parsing), \(\mathrm{BLAKE3} + \mathrm{Ed25519}\)
provides constant-time cryptographic attestation via
\texttt{cta\_\allowbreak{}fleet\_\allowbreak{}rust}, \(V_{\mathrm{gate}}\) enforces deterministic
structural and isolated sandbox invariants (\texttt{0} access to
held-out test oracles; \texttt{Falsifier\ 3}), and \(\theta_b\) wakes
organ \(b\) strictly when upstream signals cross threshold under a
Successive Shortest Path (SSP) minimum-cost flow schedule calibrated on
\texttt{15} held-out calibration probes
(\texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut\_\allowbreak{}10camp.\allowbreak{}json}, \texttt{0} overlap with the
\texttt{100} test tasks). Verified solutions from deductive and
open-world synthesis regimes are \textbf{crystallized downward online}
into the canonical De Bruijn episodic case store (\texttt{C5}).

We evaluate all \texttt{10} camps solo (\texttt{C1}--\texttt{C10})
against Arm 11 (\texttt{DES-10}) across a frozen \texttt{100}-task
deterministic-oracle corpus
(\texttt{corpus\_\allowbreak{}sha256\ =\ 79b7f751\allowbreak{}2c3a5e18\allowbreak{}eb8a13ed\allowbreak{}5323d780\allowbreak{}3a9538de\allowbreak{}cf665a21\allowbreak{}3ced6abd\allowbreak{}f87eb8d2},
\texttt{20} tasks per regime, \texttt{1,100} episodes per host =
\texttt{2,200} hardware-instrumented episodes total), invoking
Generative Pre-trained Transformer Open-Source Series
(\texttt{GPT-\allowbreak{}OSS-\allowbreak{}120B}, \texttt{temperature\ =\ 0.\allowbreak{}0}) over remote
Application Programming Interface (API) calls accounted in United States
dollars (USD) for \texttt{C1}, \texttt{C9}, and \texttt{DES-10},
executed on \textbf{both} \texttt{Intel\ Core\ i9-\allowbreak{}13900H} (\texttt{RAPL}
\texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}) and \texttt{NVIDIA\ Jetson\ AGX\ Thor}
(\texttt{TI\ INA3221} \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} and \texttt{INA238}
\texttt{VIN} physical shunt \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}, CPU-only):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{R1} (\texttt{20} tasks --- Episodic Alpha-Renamed
  Zipfian Recurrence)}: Permuted and alpha-renamed instances of
  \texttt{8} prior canonical schemas (\texttt{16} tasks) plus \texttt{4}
  recurrences of newly encountered \texttt{R3}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{R5} schemas
  testing online crystallization.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{R2} (\texttt{20} tasks --- Bitvector, Boolean Gate,
  and Protocol Framing)}: \texttt{6}-to-\texttt{10}-bit quorum parity,
  controller area network stuff-bit guards, \texttt{3}-state Mealy
  header/end-of-frame FSMs, and \texttt{LUT6} cascades (\texttt{16}-word
  bit-packed outputs).
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{R3} (\texttt{20} tasks --- Exact Deductive /
  Arithmetic / FD-SAT Constraints)}: \texttt{5}-variable
  pairwise-distinct integer systems over \texttt{1..8} combining
  ordering, weighted linear Diophantine, modular congruence, and bitwise
  exclusive-OR clauses with unique witnesses.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{R4} (\texttt{20} tasks --- Structural Causal
  \texttt{do(X=x)} and Non-Myopic Horizon Planning)}: \texttt{10}
  Simpson's-paradox confounded causal DAGs
  (\texttt{\textbar{}P(Y=1\textbar{}X=1)\ -\ P(Y=1\textbar{}do(X=1))\textbar{}\ \textgreater{}=\ 0.18})
  plus \texttt{10} \texttt{6}-step deceptive-valley Markov decision
  process (MDP) trees where greedy \texttt{t=0} reward leads to late
  \texttt{-72} penalties while a \texttt{-5} bridge toll unlocks a
  \texttt{+85..+112} summit.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{R5} (\texttt{20} tasks --- Open-World
  Specification-to-Program Synthesis)}: Standalone Python 3 synthesis
  from NL specifications and \texttt{2} public input/output (I/O)
  examples (covering chunked run-length encoding, balanced escaped
  delimiters, lexicographic cycle-detecting topological sort, exact
  Farey fraction reduction, tie-broken knapsack, generalized non-coprime
  Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT), least-recently-used (LRU) cache
  simulation, nested bracket decoding, \texttt{k}-wall grid shortest
  paths, Horner modular derivatives, and weighted interval scheduling),
  scored post-commit on \texttt{10} held-out edge-case and
  state-isolation vectors.
\end{itemize}

*On \texttt{Jetson\ AGX\ Thor} (\texttt{Python\ 3.\allowbreak{}12}), \texttt{C4}
scored \texttt{10\%} pass (\texttt{R3:\ 0/20}, \texttt{R4:\ 10/\allowbreak{}20}) and
\texttt{20\%} false commits; all other \texttt{10} arms produced
identical task verdicts across both hosts. Remote Application
Programming Interface (API) server joules are \texttt{not\_\allowbreak{}available}
and kept strictly separate from local \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} and
\texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} via explicit token and United States dollars (USD)
columns.

Four quantitative results establish the enzymatic synthesis thesis
(\texttt{Falsifiers\ 4} and \texttt{5}):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Every siloed paradigm hits a regime ceiling at or below
  \texttt{49\%} when isolated}: Deterministic specialists achieve
  \texttt{100\%} precision (\texttt{0\%} false commits) and
  \texttt{1,975} to \texttt{6,\allowbreak{}506\ tasks/\allowbreak{}J} Coherence per Joule (CpJ)
  inside their native niches---\texttt{C5} on known \texttt{R1}
  recurrences (\texttt{16/20}), \texttt{C8} on \texttt{R2} boolean/FSM
  bitvectors (\texttt{20/20}), \texttt{C2} on \texttt{R3} multi-clause
  constraints (\texttt{20/20}), and \texttt{C7} on \texttt{R4}
  Simpson's-paradox \texttt{do(X=1)} interventions
  (\texttt{10/10})---yet refuse \texttt{70\%} to \texttt{90\%} of the
  broader suite. Conversely, single-shot \texttt{C1}
  (\texttt{GPT-\allowbreak{}OSS-\allowbreak{}120B}) solves \texttt{18/20} open-world \texttt{R5}
  synthesis tasks but hallucinates bit-packed integers and near-miss
  constraint witnesses on \texttt{R2} (\texttt{1/20}) and \texttt{R3}
  (\texttt{1/20}), yielding \texttt{48\%} verified pass and
  \texttt{52\%} false commits. Unverified \texttt{C3} EBM scoring
  (\texttt{6/15} on calibration probes) collapses to \texttt{0/100}
  (\texttt{100\%} false commits) out of sample by selecting confounded
  observational and greedy-valley attractors, while myopic \texttt{C10}
  sandbox hill-climbing (\texttt{4\%} pass) stays trapped in
  \texttt{R4}'s \texttt{a\_\allowbreak{}0\ =\ 0} deceptive valley (\texttt{0/10}).
\item
  \textbf{Unattested natural-language debate (\texttt{C9}) multiplies
  energy and tokens \texttt{3x}--\texttt{3.8x} for \texttt{+1\%}
  accuracy}: Passing conversational prose across
  \texttt{Proposer\ -\textgreater{}\ Critic\ -\textgreater{}\ Judge}
  (\texttt{300} LLM calls) inflates mean prompt length \texttt{2.21x}
  across hops
  (\texttt{328.3\ -\textgreater{}\ 415.0\ -\textgreater{}\ 726.8} input
  tokens, \texttt{253,968} total tokens), raises end-to-end local energy
  to \texttt{10,\allowbreak{}913.\allowbreak{}22\ J} (\texttt{i9-13900H} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}) and
  \texttt{16,\allowbreak{}541.\allowbreak{}19\ J} (\texttt{Jetson\ Thor}
  \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j};
  \texttt{84,\allowbreak{}483.\allowbreak{}80\ J} \texttt{VIN}), yet lifts pass rate from
  \texttt{48\%} to \texttt{49\%} while still committing \texttt{51\%}
  false answers (\texttt{20/20} wrong on \texttt{R3}, \texttt{19/20}
  wrong on \texttt{R2}).
\item
  \textbf{Digital Enzymes lower inter-camp handoff energy and latency by
  \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 16,000x} to \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 155,000x}
  (\texttt{Falsifier\ 5})}: On \texttt{Intel\ Core\ i9-\allowbreak{}13900H}
  (\texttt{RAPL} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}), a \texttt{1\ KiB} in-process
  Rust Digital Enzyme verification takes \texttt{23.00\ µs} and
  \texttt{0.689\ mJ} (\texttt{44.43\ µs} and \texttt{1.140\ mJ} across a
  cross-process stdio sign-and-verify pipe), compared against
  \texttt{3,\allowbreak{}577.\allowbreak{}58\ ms} and \texttt{36.38\ J} for a live
  \texttt{Camp\ 9} NL serialization and multi-hop LLM re-parse---a
  \textbf{\texttt{155,554x} latency speedup (\texttt{80,522x} pipe)} and
  \textbf{\texttt{52,815x} socket energy reduction (\texttt{31,921x}
  pipe)}. On \texttt{NVIDIA\ Jetson\ AGX\ Thor} (\texttt{INA3221}
  \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}), Digital Enzyme
  handoff measures \texttt{49.95\ µs} and \texttt{0.732\ mJ}
  (\texttt{3.591\ mJ} \texttt{INA238} \texttt{VIN}; \texttt{186.\allowbreak{}98\ µs}
  and \texttt{3.406\ mJ} \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} over stdio pipe)
  versus \texttt{3,\allowbreak{}938.\allowbreak{}49\ ms} and \textbf{\texttt{55.14\ J}
  \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} (\texttt{281.61\ J}
  \texttt{INA238} \texttt{VIN} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j})} for live NL
  handoff---a \textbf{\texttt{78,855x} latency speedup (\texttt{21,064x}
  pipe)} and \textbf{\texttt{75,374x} physical DC shunt energy reduction
  (\texttt{16,189x} pipe)}.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{DES-10} unites all ten organs immediately
  (\texttt{98\%} pass, \texttt{1\%} false commits,
  \texttt{9.3x}--\texttt{27.1x} higher \texttt{CpJ})}: Guided by its
  epoch-\texttt{0} \texttt{SSP} schedule (\texttt{3.24\ µs} on
  \texttt{i9-13900H}, \texttt{14.20\ µs} on \texttt{Thor}) and
  \texttt{225} attested JavaScript Object Notation (JSON) / binary
  \texttt{DE10} enzyme receipts, \texttt{DES-10} routes
  \texttt{R1}--\texttt{R4} (\texttt{80} tasks) through \texttt{C5},
  \texttt{C8}, \texttt{C7}, \texttt{C2}, and \texttt{C4\ +\ C2}
  (\texttt{80\ /\allowbreak{}\ 80\ =\ 100\%} pass, \texttt{0} false commits,
  \texttt{0} LLM calls), crystallizes \texttt{4} verified
  \texttt{R3}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{R5} schemas online into \texttt{C5} to lift
  \texttt{R1} from \texttt{16/20} (\texttt{C5} solo) to \texttt{20/20},
  filters \texttt{3/20} \texttt{R5} tasks locally via
  \texttt{C3\ +\ C6\ +\ C10} (\texttt{0} LLM calls), and wakes
  \texttt{C1} strictly on the remaining \texttt{17} \texttt{R5}
  synthesis tasks (\texttt{22} total calls, \texttt{12,745} tokens,
  \texttt{\textbackslash{}\$0.0067}). Relative to \texttt{C1} solo
  (\texttt{48\%}) and \texttt{C9} NL debate (\texttt{49\%}),
  \texttt{DES-10} (\texttt{98\%}) doubles verified accuracy, cuts false
  commits by \texttt{51x}--\texttt{52x}, cuts tokens by \texttt{5.27x}
  (\texttt{C1}) and \texttt{19.93x} (\texttt{C9}), and increases
  end-to-end Coherence per Joule by \textbf{\texttt{9.25x}
  (\texttt{i9-13900H} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}) / \texttt{9.26x}
  (\texttt{Thor} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}) over \texttt{C1}} and
  \textbf{\texttt{27.13x} (\texttt{i9-13900H} \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}) /
  \texttt{26.87x} (\texttt{Thor} \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}) over
  \texttt{C9}}.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{6. Resolving the 10 Siloed AI Camps via Digital Enzymatic
Engineering \& the Testable Forcing-Function
Blueprint}\label{resolving-the-10-siloed-ai-camps-via-digital-enzymatic-engineering-the-testable-forcing-function-blueprint}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{6.1 Atlas of the 10 Siloed AI Camps
(\texttt{C1} to \texttt{C10}) and the Refutation of
Monocultures}{6.1 Atlas of the 10 Siloed AI Camps (C1 to C10) and the Refutation of Monocultures}}\label{atlas-of-the-10-siloed-ai-camps-c1-to-c10-and-the-refutation-of-monocultures}

Seventy-six years of AI research (\texttt{AI01} to \texttt{AI38})
produced ten distinct computational paradigms (\texttt{C1} through
\texttt{C10}), each optimizing one component of intelligence while
remaining blind or thermodynamically wasteful outside its native niche
(\texttt{Figure\ 7}, left panel):


\begin{center}
\openiefig{fig/communication-avalanche-fig07-ten-siloed-ai-camps-map.png}{0.94}{https://research.openie.dev/figures/communication-avalanche/ten-siloed-ai-camps-map.svg}
\end{center}


\emph{\textbf{Figure 7.} Atlas of the 10 siloed artificial intelligence
camps (\texttt{C1} to \texttt{C10}) versus Digital Enzymatic Synthesis
(\texttt{DES-10}). Every isolated monoculture fails outside its
structural regime; sub-millijoule Digital Enzymes wire all 10 into one
thresholded Successive Shortest Path (\texttt{SSP}) min-cost flow
cascade with downward skill crystallization (\texttt{CTA-T32} to
\texttt{CTA-T45}).}

\texttt{\textasciitilde{}10} to \texttt{50+\ J} / query & \textbf{Tier-3
Open-World Proposer} (woken strictly on
\texttt{V\_u\ \textgreater{}=\ theta\_u} deterministic miss) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C2} · Deductive Symbolic / \texttt{SMT} / \texttt{Lean}}
& \texttt{AI03}, \texttt{AI06}, \texttt{AI08}, \texttt{AI32},
\texttt{AI37} & Sound machine-checked proofs and \texttt{0.00\%}
false-commit formal guarantees & Cannot ingest raw ambiguous prose or
invent open-world perceptual priors solo & \texttt{0.4} to
\texttt{49\ mJ} / check & \textbf{Tier-2 Deterministic Kernel \&
Terminal Soundness Gate} (\texttt{RCE}, \texttt{Lean\ 4},
\texttt{AST}) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C3} · Latent \texttt{JEPA} / \texttt{FormEBM}} &
\texttt{AI11}, \texttt{AI16}, \texttt{AI29}, \texttt{AI37} & Fast
millijoule continuous plausibility ranking (\texttt{0.8922}
\texttt{AUROC} on unseen \texttt{Forms}) & Continuous energy minima
cannot alone certify exact discrete edge-case correctness &
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}5} to \texttt{47.09\ mJ} / rank &
\textbf{Tier-3 Pre-Filter Gate} (prunes \texttt{2.39x} invalid
candidates before checker/rollout) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C4} · \texttt{RL} / \texttt{MCTS} Tree Search} &
\texttt{AI14}, \texttt{AI18}, \texttt{AI20}, \texttt{AI23},
\texttt{AI34} & Non-myopic multi-step sequential lookahead escaping
greedy local traps & Burns megajoules on high-variance rollouts without
a fast learned value/energy prior & \texttt{1} to \texttt{10\^{}3+\ J} /
episode & \textbf{Tier-3 Sequential Planner} (guided by \texttt{C3}
\texttt{FormEBM} priors and \texttt{C2} terminal rules) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C5} · Episodic \texttt{RAG} / \texttt{Shape\ D}} &
\texttt{AI02}, \texttt{AI20}, \texttt{AI26}, \texttt{AI35} &
Sub-microsecond \(O(1)\) \texttt{1.77\ µJ} memory-mapped verified recall
(\texttt{DNULLMM1}) & Cosine \texttt{RAG} retrieves invalid neighbors;
exact \texttt{Shape\ D} misses on novel structures & \texttt{1.77\ µJ}
(\texttt{mmap}) to \texttt{\textasciitilde{}50\ mJ} & \textbf{Tier-1
Frozen Verified Case Store} (absorbs recurring Zipfian head at zero
\texttt{FLOPs}) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C6} · Evolutionary Code Search} & \texttt{AI05},
\texttt{AI31} (\texttt{FunSearch}) & Population diversity escapes
gradient/prompt local optima via code mutation & Wastes thousands of
sampler calls without sub-millisecond fitness pre-screening &
\texttt{10} to \texttt{10\^{}3\ J} / island & \textbf{Tier-3
Combinatorial Explorer} (crystallizing winning programs downward into
\texttt{C2}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{C5}) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C7} · Causal Do-Calculus / \texttt{DAGs}} &
\texttt{AI13} (Pearl \texttt{DAGs}), \texttt{AI24} (\texttt{MPNN}) &
Exact interventional \(P(Y \mid \mathrm{do}(X))\) deconfounding and
backdoor adjustment & Requires a typed causal graph schema; cannot
extract variables from raw text solo & \texttt{0.5} to \texttt{10\ mJ} /
query & \textbf{Tier-2 Causal \& Graphical Kernel} (eliminates spurious
correlation before policy commit) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C8} · Sub-Symbolic / \texttt{SSM} / \texttt{LUT6}} &
\texttt{AI04}, \texttt{AI15}, \texttt{AI17}, \texttt{AI22},
\texttt{AI36} & Single-cycle \texttt{4\ ns} combinational \texttt{LUT6}
(\texttt{81\ ×\ RAMB36E2}, \texttt{\textasciitilde{}12\ pJ}
\texttt{est\_j}, \texttt{0} \texttt{FLOPs}) & Bounded Boolean input
width per page; cannot synthesize novel multi-hop algorithms solo &
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}12\ pJ} \texttt{est\_j} (\texttt{FPGA}) &
\textbf{Tier-0 Physical Reflex Floor} (executes crystallized hot-path
predicates in \texttt{1} cycle) \\
\textbf{\texttt{C9} · Multi-Agent \texttt{NL} Debate} & \texttt{AI09},
\texttt{AI10}, \texttt{AI28}, \texttt{AI30}, \texttt{AI33} & Generates
diverse multi-perspective candidate decompositions across specialized
roles & Unattested prose relays suffer \texttt{3.71x} prompt bloat and
\texttt{13.33\%} false commits (\texttt{CTA-E2}) &
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}95} to \texttt{110+\ J} / task &
\textbf{Replaced as Transport}; retained strictly as parallel attested
\texttt{Phi\_IR} proposers \\
\textbf{\texttt{C10} · Embodied Sandbox / Control} & \texttt{AI02},
\texttt{AI27}, \texttt{AI28}, \texttt{AI31}, \texttt{AI38} & Grounded
physical/execution feedback and repeat-call state-leak falsification &
Unsafe or slow when executing unscreened raw proposals without upstream
type/hash gates & \texttt{1.12} to \texttt{25\ mJ} / oracle &
\textbf{Terminal Behavioral Oracle} (\texttt{diff\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}kernel}
enforcing \texttt{0} state-leak commits) \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{6.2 Formal Theory of Digital Enzymes and
10-Camp Pareto Dominance (\texttt{CTA-T32} to
\texttt{CTA-T40})}{6.2 Formal Theory of Digital Enzymes and 10-Camp Pareto Dominance (CTA-T32 to CTA-T40)}}\label{formal-theory-of-digital-enzymes-and-10-camp-pareto-dominance-cta-t32-to-cta-t40}

In biochemistry, metabolic pathways across incompatible chemical
intermediates remain kinetically frozen until \textbf{enzymes} bind
substrates into a precise transition-state geometry, slash activation
energy \(E^\ddagger\) by orders of magnitude, enforce stereochemical
specificity, and exit the reaction \textbf{completely unconsumed}.
Contemporary multi-agent AI suffers the exact digital analog of
uncatalyzed thermal reactions: bridging paradigm camp \(a\) to camp
\(b\) via free-form natural-language prose (\texttt{Camp\ C9},
\texttt{Arm\ C} in \texttt{CTA-E2}) incurs retry-multiplied
serialization/parsing energy \(k_{\text{retry}} \cdot c_{\text{nl}}\)
(\texttt{\textasciitilde{}15} to \texttt{110\ J}), \texttt{3.71x}
context inflation, and per-hop semantic drift \(\epsilon > 0\)
(\texttt{CTA-T1}).

Module \texttt{Avalanche/\allowbreak{}EnzymaticSynthesis.\allowbreak{}lean} (\texttt{{[}T{]}})
defines a \textbf{Digital Enzyme}
\(\mathcal{E}_{a \to b} = (\Phi_{\text{IR}},\, \mathrm{attest\_ok},\, V_{\text{det}},\, \mathrm{decode},\, c_{\text{enz}})\)
between source camp state \(\mathcal{S}_a\) and recipient camp state
\(\mathcal{S}_b\):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Canonical Substrate Encoder
  (\(\Phi_{\text{IR}} : \mathcal{S}_a \to \mathcal{I}_{ab}\))}: Projects
  camp \(a\)'s internal state into a minimal, schema-typed intermediate
  representation (\texttt{AST} patch diff, \texttt{RCE} canonical
  \texttt{Form}, \texttt{DAG} backdoor tuple, or \texttt{DNULLMM1} key),
  stripping conversational commentary
  (\texttt{3.71x\ -\textgreater{}\ 1.00x}).
\item
  \textbf{Constant-Time Attestation + Deterministic Gate
  (\(\mathrm{attest\_ok} \wedge V_{\text{det}} : \mathcal{I}_{ab} \to \{0,1\}\))}:
  Verifies \texttt{256}-bit \texttt{BLAKE3} integrity, \texttt{64}-byte
  \texttt{Ed25519} sender authorization, and deterministic structural
  admissibility at total overhead \(c_{\text{enz}} < 1\text{ mJ}\)
  (\texttt{643–926\ µJ} in \texttt{CTA-E1}).
\item
  \textbf{Catalyst State Invariance \& Sequential Composition
  (\texttt{CTA-T32} to \texttt{CTA-T35})}: Across any sequence of
  \(M \ge 1\) reactions (\texttt{runEnzymaticChain}), the enzyme
  instance returns structurally unchanged
  (\(\mathcal{E}' = \mathcal{E}\), zero weight mutation), lowers
  per-handoff activation energy by
  \(\Delta E^\ddagger = k_{\text{retry}} c_{\text{nl}} - c_{\text{enz}} > 0\)
  whenever \(c_{\text{enz}} < c_{\text{nl}}\) (\texttt{CTA-T32}), saves
  \(M \cdot \Delta E^\ddagger\) joules linearly across \(M\) handoffs
  (\texttt{CTA-T33}), propagates \textbf{zero} unverified semantic drift
  compared to \(M\epsilon > 0\) uncatalyzed prose drift
  (\texttt{CTA-T34}), and composes associatively across multi-organ
  chains \(\mathcal{E}_2 \circ \mathcal{E}_1\) with additive savings
  \(\Delta E^\ddagger_1 + \Delta E^\ddagger_2\) (\texttt{CTA-T35}).
\end{enumerate}

Equipped with \(< 1\text{ mJ}\) Digital Enzymes across \(K \ge 2\)
paradigm camps (instantiated at \(K = \mathrm{TenCamps} = 10\)) and
\(R \ge 1\) task regimes with verified pass indicator
\(p(k, r) \in \{0, 1\}\) and execution joules \(c(k, r) > 0\), the
executable \textbf{Enzymatic Niche Router} (\texttt{selectNicheCamp})
selects:

\[k^*(r) \in \arg\min_{k \in \{1,\dots,K\}} \bigl\{\, c(k, r) \;\big|\; p(k, r) = 1 \,\bigr\}, \qquad E_{\text{DES}}(r) = c\!\left(k^*(r),\, r\right) + c_{\text{enz}}.\]

Five kernel-checked theorems establish why \textbf{Digital Enzymatic
Synthesis (\texttt{DES-10})} strictly dominates every siloed AI
monoculture (\texttt{{[}T{]}}):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Coverage Union (\texttt{CTA-T36})}: For every regime \(r\) and
  solo camp \(k_0\),
  \(\mathrm{Pass}_{\text{DES}}(r) = \bigvee_{k=1}^K p(k, r) \ge p(k_0, r)\)
  and \(c(k^*(r), r) \le c(k_0, r)\) whenever \(p(k_0, r) = 1\).
\item
  \textbf{Strict Global Pass Superiority --- Refutation of Monocultures
  (\texttt{CTA-T37})}: Whenever every regime \(r \in \{1,\dots,R\}\) is
  solved by at least one specialist camp while no single solo camp
  solves all \(R\) regimes (\(\forall k,\, \exists r,\, p(k, r) = 0\)),
  \texttt{DES-10} achieves full global coverage \(R\) and strictly beats
  every solo camp:
  \(\sum_r p(k_0, r) < \sum_r \mathrm{Pass}_{\text{DES}}(r) = R\).
\item
  \textbf{Strict Total-Joule \& \texttt{CpJ} Dominance
  (\texttt{CTA-T38}, \texttt{CTA-T39})}: Even against an idealized
  universal camp \(k_{\text{univ}}\) solving all \(R\) regimes (such as
  a frontier model invoked on every syntax, scope, causal, and proof
  task), \texttt{DES-10} expends strictly fewer total joules
  (\(E_{\text{DES}} < E_{\text{solo}}\)) and achieves strictly higher
  Coherence per Joule
  (\(\mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{DES}} > \mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{solo}}\)) whenever
  cumulative niche savings exceed routing overhead:
  \[\sum_{r=1}^{R} \bigl(c(k_{\text{univ}}, r) - c(k^*(r), r)\bigr) > R \cdot c_{\text{enz}}.\]
  On \texttt{CTA-E2} (\texttt{60} tasks),
  \texttt{c\_enz\ \textless{}\ 1\ mJ} while
  \(c(k_{\text{univ}}, r) - c(k^*(r), r) \approx 15\text{ J}\) across
  the \texttt{40} Category-1/2 tasks --- exceeding the \texttt{CTA-T38}
  threshold by \texttt{\textgreater{}\ 10,000x}.
\item
  \textbf{Zero-Wait One-Pass Calibration (\texttt{CTA-T40})}:
  Identifying \(k^*(r)\) requires \textbf{zero gradient-training epochs}
  --- a single deterministic profiling pass of \(K \cdot m\) probes
  (\(m \ge 1\)) populates \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json} and immediately
  constructively certifies \(k^*(r)\).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 4: Ten-Camp Digital Enzymatic
Synthesis (\texttt{DES-10}) on a Confounded-Causal + Exact-Integer +
Code-Generation
Task}{Worked Example 4: Ten-Camp Digital Enzymatic Synthesis (DES-10) on a Confounded-Causal + Exact-Integer + Code-Generation Task}}\label{worked-example-4-ten-camp-digital-enzymatic-synthesis-des-10-on-a-confounded-causal-exact-integer-code-generation-task}

Consider a composite operational triage query that couples three
distinct reasoning modes: (a) natural-language incident logs
stratification-confounded across workload severity
\(Z \in \{\text{Light}, \text{Heavy}\}\) (\texttt{50\%} base prevalence
each), (b) exact rational interventional estimation
\(P(Y=1 \mid \mathrm{do}(X=x))\), and (c) Python integer quota
allocation
\texttt{allocate\_quota(budget\_units:\ int)\ -\textgreater{}\ tuple{[}int,\ int{]}}:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Observed Stratified Counts (\texttt{800} Total Episodes)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Under \(Z = \text{Light}\): \texttt{Policy\ X=1} succeeds on
    \texttt{81\ /\ 90} (\texttt{90.00\%}), whereas \texttt{Policy\ X=0}
    succeeds on \texttt{266\ /\allowbreak{}\ 310} (\texttt{85.81\%}).
  \item
    Under \(Z = \text{Heavy}\): \texttt{Policy\ X=1} succeeds on
    \texttt{192\ /\allowbreak{}\ 310} (\texttt{61.94\%}), whereas
    \texttt{Policy\ X=0} succeeds on \texttt{50\ /\ 90}
    (\texttt{55.56\%}).
  \item
    \textbf{Confounded Pooled Trap}: Because operators routed
    \texttt{310\ /\allowbreak{}\ 400} (\texttt{77.5\%}) of \texttt{X=1} runs onto
    \texttt{Heavy} incidents, naive unadjusted conditioning reverses the
    true effect:
    \(P(Y=1 \mid X=1) = 273 / 400 = 68.25\% < P(Y=1 \mid X=0) = 316 / 400 = 79.00\%\)
    (Simpson's paradox).
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Why Isolated AI Camps Fail Solo}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{\texttt{Camp\ 1} (Autoregressive \texttt{LLM} Solo,
    \texttt{\textasciitilde{}15.0\ J})}: Compares pooled empirical rates
    \texttt{79.00\%\ \textgreater{}\ 68.25\%} or rounds floating-point
    intermediates inconsistently, recommending inferior
    \texttt{Policy\ X=0} (\texttt{-5.29} percentage points true
    interventional loss).
  \item
    \textbf{\texttt{Camp\ 2} (Deductive Symbolic / Satisfiability Modulo
    Theories (\texttt{SMT}) Solo)}: Requires a pre-formalized
    \texttt{DAG} and exact rational constraints; rejects raw prose
    incident logs at input parse.
  \item
    \textbf{\texttt{Camp\ 5} (Episodic Retrieval-Augmented Generation
    (\texttt{RAG}) / Binary Memory-Mapped Store (\texttt{DNULLMM1})
    Solo, \texttt{1.77\ µJ})}: Computes canonical structure hash on the
    unseen
    \texttt{(Z\ -\textgreater{}\ X,\ Z\ -\textgreater{}\ Y,\ X\ -\textgreater{}\ Y)}
    variable schema, observes a lookup \texttt{miss}, and abstains.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{How \texttt{DES-10} Solves the Composite Task via
  \texttt{\textless{}\ 1\ mJ} Digital Enzymes}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{1} (\texttt{C5} Probe -\textgreater{}
    \texttt{C1} Schema Parser)}: \texttt{DNULLMM1} lookup
    (\texttt{1.77\ µJ}) returns a \texttt{miss} and triggers
    \texttt{eventfd} wakeup of a constrained \texttt{C1} grammar parser
    (\texttt{parse\_\allowbreak{}kernel} + schema extractor) that emits canonical
    intermediate representation (\texttt{IR})
    \(\Phi_{\text{IR}} = (\text{DAG}: \{Z \to X, Z \to Y, X \to Y\},\; \text{table}: ((81,90),(266,310),(192,310),(50,90)))\)
    without prose commentary.
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{2} (Digital Enzyme \(\mathcal{E}_{1 \to 7}\):
    \texttt{643\ µJ}, \texttt{26.0\ µs})}: Verifies \texttt{BLAKE3} +
    \texttt{Ed25519} signature, checks \texttt{DAG} acyclicity, and
    confirms exact row-sum conservation (\texttt{400\ +\ 400\ =\ 800}).
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{3} (\texttt{C7} Do-Calculus Backdoor Kernel:
    \texttt{0.64\ mJ})}: Identifies \(\{Z\}\) as a valid backdoor
    adjustment set blocking \(X \leftarrow Z \to Y\) and evaluates exact
    rational interventional success:
    \[P(Y=1 \mid \mathrm{do}(X=1)) = \frac{1}{2}\!\left(\frac{81}{90} + \frac{192}{310}\right) = \frac{471}{620} \approx 75.97\%, \qquad P(Y=1 \mid \mathrm{do}(X=0)) = \frac{1}{2}\!\left(\frac{266}{310} + \frac{50}{90}\right) = \frac{986}{1{,}395} \approx 70.68\%,\]
    yielding exact positive causal lift
    \(\Delta_{\text{do}} = \frac{471}{620} - \frac{986}{1{,}395} = +\frac{295}{5{,}580} \approx +5.29\%\)
    strictly favoring \texttt{X=1}.
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{4} (Digital Enzyme \(\mathcal{E}_{7 \to 2}\) +
    \texttt{C2} Exact Witness + \texttt{C10} Sandbox Gate:
    \texttt{18.15\ mJ})}: \(\mathcal{E}_{7 \to 2}\) (\texttt{651\ µJ})
    passes \texttt{(\allowbreak{}x\_\allowbreak{}opt\ =\ 1,\allowbreak{}\ lift\ =\ 295/\allowbreak{}5580)} to \texttt{C2}
    deterministic code synthesis (\texttt{15.73\ mJ}), which emits
    integer-conserving \texttt{allocate\_\allowbreak{}quota}, verified across
    \texttt{8} boundary budgets (\texttt{0,\allowbreak{}\ 1,\allowbreak{}\ 7,\allowbreak{}\ 100,\allowbreak{}\ .\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}}) by
    \texttt{C10} \texttt{diff\_\allowbreak{}verifier\_\allowbreak{}kernel} (\texttt{1.12\ mJ},
    \texttt{0} state leaks; total local kernel + enzyme pipeline =
    \textbf{\texttt{19.44\ mJ}}, \texttt{\textless{}\ 50\ mJ}).
  \item
    \textbf{Step \texttt{5} (Downward Crystallization into
    \texttt{C5})}: The verified canonical mapping crystallizes into
    \texttt{DNULLMM1}, so every future arrival of this confounded triage
    schema resolves in \textbf{\texttt{1.77\ µJ}} (\texttt{0} parser or
    solver invocations).
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{6.3 The 5-Loop Testable Forcing-Function
Blueprint and 4-Tier Downward Crystallization (\texttt{CTA-T41} to
\texttt{CTA-T45})}{6.3 The 5-Loop Testable Forcing-Function Blueprint and 4-Tier Downward Crystallization (CTA-T41 to CTA-T45)}}\label{the-5-loop-testable-forcing-function-blueprint-and-4-tier-downward-crystallization-cta-t41-to-cta-t45}

Static routing across the ten camps captures immediate single-task
savings (\texttt{CTA-T38}), but biological and cultural evolution
achieve compound exponential gains through a \textbf{closed-loop
downward forcing function}: high-energy exploratory solutions are
verified once and progressively compiled into lower-energy deterministic
substrates (\texttt{Figure\ 8}). Let the four execution tiers carry
strictly ordered per-arrival energies \(c_3 > c_2 > c_1 > c_0 > 0\)
(\texttt{FourTierCosts} in \texttt{EnzymaticSynthesis.\allowbreak{}lean}):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Tier 3 (\texttt{c\_3\ \textasciitilde{}\ 10–50+\ J} · Frontier
  Synthesis \& Search)}: Camps \texttt{C1}, \texttt{C4}, and \texttt{C6}
  (\texttt{LLMs}, \texttt{MCTS}, evolutionary code islands) pre-screened
  by \texttt{C3} (\texttt{FormEBM}, \texttt{47.09\ mJ}).
\item
  \textbf{Tier 2 (\texttt{c\_2\ \textasciitilde{}\ 0.4–49\ mJ} ·
  Verified Symbolic \& Causal Rules)}: Camps \texttt{C2} and \texttt{C7}
  (\texttt{AST}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{SMT} repair kernels, \texttt{RCE} canonical
  checker, \texttt{DAG} backdoor adjustment).
\item
  \textbf{Tier 1 (\texttt{c\_1\ \textasciitilde{}\ 1.77\ µJ} · Frozen
  Episodic Case Store)}: Camp \texttt{C5} (\texttt{Shape\ D}
  \texttt{DNULLMM1} zero-copy \texttt{POSIX\ mmap} hash table).
\item
  \textbf{Tier 0 (\texttt{c\_0\ \textasciitilde{}\ 12\ pJ}
  \texttt{est\_j} · Single-Cycle Hardware Automaton)}: Camp \texttt{C8}
  (\texttt{81\ ×\ RAMB36E2} Beneš-routed \texttt{LUT6} pages on
  \texttt{xck26}, \texttt{0} \texttt{DSP48E2} slices).
\end{itemize}


\begin{center}
\openiefig{fig/communication-avalanche-fig08-digital-enzymatic-forcing-function.png}{0.94}{https://research.openie.dev/figures/communication-avalanche/digital-enzymatic-forcing-function.svg}
\end{center}


\emph{\textbf{Figure 8.} Computational reaction-coordinate barrier
lowering (\textbf{left}, \texttt{CTA-T32} to \texttt{CTA-T35}) and the
5-loop closed-loop downward forcing-function blueprint (\textbf{right},
\texttt{CTA-T41} to \texttt{CTA-T45}) driving recurring workloads from
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}15\ J} Tier-3 synthesis down to
\texttt{1.77\ µJ} episodic recall and \texttt{\textasciitilde{}12\ pJ}
\texttt{est\_j} single-cycle \texttt{LUT6} reflexes.}

The \textbf{5-Loop Testable Forcing-Function Blueprint} wires all ten
camps into a self-tightening thermodynamic ratchet:

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Loop\ 1} --- Universal Attested Envelope
  (\texttt{Phi\_IR} + \texttt{BLAKE3} + \texttt{Ed25519})}: Every
  inter-camp edge replaces free-form conversational text with a
  canonical \texttt{Phi\_IR} payload carrying a \texttt{256}-bit
  \texttt{BLAKE3} digest and \texttt{64}-byte \texttt{Ed25519} signature
  (\texttt{25.\allowbreak{}95–37.\allowbreak{}39\ µs}, \texttt{643–926\ µJ}, \texttt{0\ /\ 200}
  Byzantine escapes in \texttt{CTA-E3},
  \texttt{CTA-T16}--\texttt{CTA-T18}).
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Loop\ 2} --- One-Pass Calibration \& \texttt{SSP}
  Niche Router}: A single \texttt{K\ *\ m} profiling pass
  (\texttt{CTA-T40}) populates \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json} (\texttt{RAPL}
  \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j} or inline shunt \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j}).
  Bellman-Ford node potentials plus Johnson reduced-cost Dijkstra
  (\texttt{SSP}) compute exact min-cost flow routes in \texttt{2.11\ µs}
  (\texttt{2.40\ µs} on node-dropout failover,
  \texttt{CTA-T24}--\texttt{CTA-T28}).
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Loop\ 3} --- Verifier-Gated Novelty Escalation
  (\texttt{eventfd} + \texttt{FormEBM})}: Downstream Tier-3 nodes block
  in the OS kernel via \texttt{eventfd} at zero CPU cycles while
  membrane potential \(V_u < \theta_u\)
  (\texttt{CTA-T7}--\texttt{CTA-T10}). Only deterministic Tier-0/1/2
  misses cross \(\theta_u = 1.0\), where \texttt{Camp\ C3}
  (\texttt{FormEBM}, \texttt{47.09\ mJ}) ranks candidate \texttt{Forms}
  (\texttt{0.8922} \texttt{AUROC}) to cut downstream checker/rollout
  invocations by \texttt{2.39x} before waking
  \texttt{C1}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{C4}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{C6}.
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Loop\ 4} --- Automatic Downward Crystallization
  (\texttt{C1/C4/C6\ -\textgreater{}\ C5\ -\textgreater{}\ C2\ -\textgreater{}\ C8})}:
  Every novel candidate must pass the terminal soundness + sandbox
  repeat-call gate (\texttt{C2} + \texttt{C10}, \texttt{0\ /\ 60} false
  commits). Once a verified structural witness recurs across threshold
  \(\tau_j\) arrivals, \texttt{promoteVerifiedTier} compiles it downward
  (\texttt{Tier\ 3\ -\textgreater{}\ Tier\ 2} rule extraction,
  \texttt{Tier\ 2\ -\textgreater{}\ Tier\ 1} \texttt{DNULLMM1} slot
  freeze, \texttt{Tier\ 1\ -\textgreater{}\ Tier\ 0} equivalence-checked
  \texttt{LUT6} truth-table page) at one-time compile cost
  \(E_{\text{comp}, j}\), preserving semantic soundness across all four
  tiers (\texttt{CTA-T44}, proved with \textbf{zero axioms}).
\item
  \textbf{\texttt{Loop\ 5} --- Hardware Rail/Counter Joule Ledger \&
  Hit-Rate Auto-Pruning}: Every firing appends a hash-chained joule
  receipt (\texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}, \texttt{core\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j},
  \texttt{psys\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}, and inline \texttt{INA238}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{INA260}
  \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} where attached) and audits each compiled skill
  over sliding window \(N\). By \textbf{Theorem \texttt{CTA-T45}
  (\texttt{downward\_\allowbreak{}compilation\_\allowbreak{}falsifier\_\allowbreak{}hit\_\allowbreak{}rate\_\allowbreak{}iff})}, a
  downward-compiled skill with \(H\) hits over \(N\) arrivals pays back
  strictly if and only if its empirical hit rate \(h = H / N\) exceeds
  the exact falsifier floor:
  \[h > h_{\min} = \frac{E_{\text{comp}}}{N \cdot (c_{\text{high}} - c_{\text{low}})}, \qquad \text{and fails to pay back iff } h \le h_{\min}.\]
  Any compiled rule with \(h \le h_{\min}\) is automatically evicted
  from \texttt{cost\_\allowbreak{}lut.\allowbreak{}json}, preventing cache bloat under
  distribution shift, while surviving skills drive marginal task energy
  monotonically from \(c_3\) down to \(c_0\) (\texttt{CTA-T41},
  \texttt{CTA-T42}).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 5: Closed-Loop Forcing
Function --- Exact \texttt{1,000}-Arrival Zipfian Downward
Crystallization Trajectory (\texttt{CTA-T23}, \texttt{CTA-T41},
\texttt{CTA-T45})}{Worked Example 5: Closed-Loop Forcing Function --- Exact 1,000-Arrival Zipfian Downward Crystallization Trajectory (CTA-T23, CTA-T41, CTA-T45)}}\label{worked-example-5-closed-loop-forcing-function-exact-1000-arrival-zipfian-downward-crystallization-trajectory-cta-t23-cta-t41-cta-t45}

Track \texttt{M\ =\ 1,\allowbreak{}000} sequential task arrivals distributed across
\texttt{K\ =\ 22} structural equivalence classes under a skewed Zipfian
workload (\texttt{4} hot finite-domain classes receiving
\texttt{260,\allowbreak{}\ 115,\allowbreak{}\ 80,\allowbreak{}\ 65} arrivals = \texttt{520} total; \texttt{18}
medium classes receiving \texttt{480} total arrivals, each seen
\(\ge 8\) times):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Four Execution Tiers and Per-Invocation Energy}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Tier \texttt{3} (Frontier \texttt{LLM} Escalation)}:
    \(c_3 = 15.000\;\text{J/task}\) (\texttt{CTA-E2} \texttt{Arm\ B}
    measured \texttt{15.30\ J} \texttt{pkg\_\allowbreak{}reported\_\allowbreak{}j}/verified task).
  \item
    \textbf{Tier \texttt{2} (Synthesized Symbolic / AST Rule)}:
    \(c_2 = 0.0150\;\text{J/task}\) (\texttt{15.0\ mJ}, matching
    \texttt{det\_\allowbreak{}patch\_\allowbreak{}kernel} \texttt{15.73\ mJ}), plus
    \texttt{\textless{}\ 0.7\ mJ} channel attestation =
    \texttt{0.0157\ J}.
  \item
    \textbf{Tier \texttt{1} (\texttt{DNULLMM1} Binary Memory-Mapped Case
    Store \texttt{{[}same\ house{]}})}:
    \(c_1 = 1.77 \times 10^{-6}\;\text{J/task}\) (\texttt{1.77\ µJ} mmap
    probe; \texttt{644.8\ µJ} with cross-process \texttt{Ed25519}
    envelope).
  \item
    \textbf{Tier \texttt{0} (\texttt{xck26} 36-Kbit Block RAM
    (\texttt{RAMB36E2})-Paged \texttt{LUT6} Circuit
    \texttt{{[}same\ house{]}})}:
    \(c_0 = 1.2 \times 10^{-11}\;\text{J/task}\)
    (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}12\ pJ} analytical \texttt{est\_j}
    single-cycle \texttt{4.0\ ns} evaluation;
    \texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ false}).
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Exact Break-Even Amortization Steps (\(N^*\), \texttt{CTA-T23}
  / \texttt{CTA-T41})}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Distilling a verified Tier-\texttt{3} solution into a reusable
    Tier-\texttt{2} rule costs
    \(E_{\text{synth},3\to 2} = 0.450\;\text{J}\), giving break-even
    repeat count:
    \[N^*_{3\to 2} = \left\lceil \frac{E_{\text{synth},3\to 2}}{c_3 - c_2} \right\rceil = \left\lceil \frac{0.450}{15.000 - 0.015} \right\rceil = \lceil 0.03003 \rceil = \mathbf{1\;\text{repeat arrival}},\]
    paying back its entire synthesis cost on the very next occurrence of
    the class.
  \item
    Freezing a \texttt{3}-times-verified Tier-\texttt{2} rule into a
    \texttt{DNULLMM1} Tier-\texttt{1} slot costs
    \(E_{\text{synth},2\to 1} = 0.045\;\text{J}\), breaking even at
    \(N^*_{2\to 1} = \lceil 0.045 / (0.015 - 0.00000177) \rceil = \mathbf{4\;\text{subsequent hits}}\).
  \item
    Compiling each of the \texttt{4} hot finite-domain classes (after
    \texttt{50} cumulative hits) into a resident \texttt{LUT6} page
    costs \(E_{\text{synth},1\to 0} = 0.250\;\text{J}\)
    (\texttt{1.000\ J} total across \texttt{4} classes).
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Cumulative \texttt{1,000}-Arrival Energy Ledger vs.~Monolithic
  Baseline}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Monolithic Tier-\texttt{3} Baseline (\texttt{1,000}
    \texttt{LLM} calls)}:
    \(E_{\text{mono}} = 1{,}000 \times 15.000\;\text{J} = \mathbf{15{,}000.00\;\text{J}}\),
    yielding
    \(\mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{mono}} = 1{,}000 / 15{,}000 = \mathbf{0.0667\;\text{tasks/J}}\).
  \item
    \textbf{Downward-Crystallizing Cascade (\texttt{1,000} verified
    solves, \texttt{0} false commits)}:

    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      \texttt{22} first-time novel class misses (Tier-\texttt{1/2} miss
      \texttt{0.002\ J} + Tier-\texttt{3} \texttt{15.000\ J} + rule
      distillation \texttt{0.450\ J}):
      \texttt{22\ x\ 15.\allowbreak{}452\ J\ =\ 339.\allowbreak{}944\ J}.
    \item
      \texttt{66} second-through-fourth arrivals across \texttt{22}
      classes on Tier \texttt{2}
      (\texttt{66\ x\ 0.\allowbreak{}0157\ J\ =\ 1.\allowbreak{}036\ J}) + \texttt{22}
      Tier-\texttt{1} \texttt{DNULLMM1} slot freezes
      (\texttt{22\ x\ 0.\allowbreak{}045\ J\ =\ 0.\allowbreak{}990\ J}): \texttt{2.026\ J}.
    \item
      \texttt{576} Tier-\texttt{1} \texttt{DNULLMM1} hits (\texttt{184}
      pre-\texttt{LUT6} hits on the \texttt{4} hot classes +
      \texttt{392} remaining hits on the \texttt{18} medium classes,
      including full \texttt{644.8\ µJ} cross-process attested
      envelopes) + \texttt{4} Tier-\texttt{0} page compilations
      (\texttt{1.000\ J}): \texttt{0.\allowbreak{}371\ J\ +\ 1.\allowbreak{}000\ J\ =\ 1.\allowbreak{}371\ J}.
    \item
      \texttt{336} post-promotion Tier-\texttt{0} \texttt{LUT6} hits on
      the \texttt{4} hot classes (\texttt{336\ x\ 12\ pJ}):
      \texttt{4.03\ nJ} (\texttt{0.\allowbreak{}000000004\ J}).
    \item
      \textbf{Total Cascade Energy}:
      \(E_{\text{cascade}} = 339.944 + 2.026 + 1.371 = \mathbf{343.34\;\text{J}}\),
      achieving
      \(\mathrm{CpJ}_{\text{cascade}} = 1{,}000 / 343.34 = \mathbf{2.913\;\text{tasks/J}}\)
      --- a \textbf{\texttt{43.69x} cumulative \texttt{CpJ}
      multiplication} over the \texttt{1,000}-task horizon and a
      \textbf{\texttt{1.25\ x\ 10\^{}\{12\}x} marginal energy drop}
      (\texttt{15.0\ J\ -\textgreater{}\ 12\ pJ} \texttt{est\_j}) on
      crystallized hot paths (\texttt{CTA-T45}).
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{6.4 Provable
\texttt{Month\ /\allowbreak{}\ Quarter\ /\allowbreak{}\ Year\ /\allowbreak{}\ Decade} Horizon Roadmap
(\texttt{HM01} to
\texttt{HD04})}{6.4 Provable Month / Quarter / Year / Decade Horizon Roadmap (HM01 to HD04)}}\label{provable-month-quarter-year-decade-horizon-roadmap-hm01-to-hd04}

Theorem \textbf{\texttt{CTA-T43}
(\texttt{horizon\_\allowbreak{}cpj\_\allowbreak{}monotonicity\_\allowbreak{}month\_\allowbreak{}quarter\_\allowbreak{}year\_\allowbreak{}decade})}
proves that once initial discovery and downward-compilation energy
\(E_{\text{init}} > 0\) is invested into verified crystallized kernels
of steady-state cost \(c_0\), cumulative Coherence per Joule
\(\mathrm{CpJ}(T) = (q \cdot T) / (E_{\text{init}} + T \cdot c_0)\)
increases \textbf{strictly monotonically} across every widening
operational timescale
\(0 < t_{\text{month}} < t_{\text{quarter}} < t_{\text{year}} < t_{\text{decade}}\):

\[\mathrm{CpJ}(t_{\text{month}}) < \mathrm{CpJ}(t_{\text{quarter}}) < \mathrm{CpJ}(t_{\text{year}}) < \mathrm{CpJ}(t_{\text{decade}}).\]

Catalog
\texttt{openie-\allowbreak{}research-\allowbreak{}experiments/\allowbreak{}communication-\allowbreak{}avalanche/\allowbreak{}catalogs/\allowbreak{}enzymatic-\allowbreak{}horizon-\allowbreak{}roadmap.\allowbreak{}csv}

pre-registered clinical/neurotechnology gates
\texttt{CA-H6}--\texttt{CA-H8} into a fine-grained falsifiable roadmap
spanning Months \texttt{1–3}, Quarters \texttt{1–4}, Years \texttt{1–5},
and Decade \texttt{1} (\texttt{2026–2036}):

\texttt{CTA-T45})}{7. Formal Verification in Lean 4.36.0 (CTA-T1 to CTA-T45)}}\label{formal-verification-in-lean-4.36.0-cta-t1-to-cta-t45}

crystallization contain zero hidden assumptions, we formalized

(\texttt{lake\ build} exit \texttt{0}, \texttt{0} warnings, \texttt{0}
\texttt{sorry}, \texttt{0} custom axioms; \texttt{4} theorems require
zero axioms of any kind and \texttt{41} use only standard Lean kernel
axioms \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.\allowbreak{}choice}, and
\texttt{Quot.\allowbreak{}sound}, \texttt{{[}T{]}}):

\section{\texorpdfstring{8. Predicted Analog Hive Mind: Neural Interface
Matrix (\texttt{BM01} to \texttt{BM18}), Opt-In \texttt{N}-Mind
Bridging, and Paracrine Secretome Audit (\texttt{EV01} to
\texttt{EV16})}{8. Predicted Analog Hive Mind: Neural Interface Matrix (BM01 to BM18), Opt-In N-Mind Bridging, and Paracrine Secretome Audit (EV01 to EV16)}}\label{predicted-analog-hive-mind-neural-interface-matrix-bm01-to-bm18-opt-in-n-mind-bridging-and-paracrine-secretome-audit-ev01-to-ev16}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{8.1 From asymmetric neural readout to
closed-loop modulation (\texttt{BM01} to
\texttt{BM18})}{8.1 From asymmetric neural readout to closed-loop modulation (BM01 to BM18)}}\label{from-asymmetric-neural-readout-to-closed-loop-modulation-bm01-to-bm18}

Catalog
\texttt{openie-\allowbreak{}research-\allowbreak{}experiments/\allowbreak{}communication-\allowbreak{}avalanche/\allowbreak{}catalogs/\allowbreak{}bci-\allowbreak{}sensing-\allowbreak{}modulation-\allowbreak{}matrix.\allowbreak{}csv}
(\texttt{BM01} to \texttt{BM18}) maps 18 real and proposed
neural/paracrine interfaces across spatial resolution, temporal latency,
information transfer rate (\texttt{ITR}), channel count, agency gating,
and clinical maturity. Contemporary human \texttt{BCIs} exhibit a
pronounced directional asymmetry (\texttt{{[}E{]}}):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{High-rate sensing (read-only output, \texttt{BM01} to
  \texttt{BM03}, \texttt{BM06} to \texttt{BM08}, \texttt{BM10})}:
  Non-invasive scalp \texttt{EEG} (\texttt{0.\allowbreak{}2-\allowbreak{}2.\allowbreak{}5\ bit/\allowbreak{}s}, Wolpaw et
  al.~2002) {[}39{]}, wearable \texttt{OPM-MEG} (\texttt{1-\allowbreak{}4\ bit/\allowbreak{}s},
  Boto et al.~2018) {[}4{]}, \texttt{fNIRS} (\texttt{0.\allowbreak{}05-\allowbreak{}0.\allowbreak{}3\ bit/\allowbreak{}s},
  Ferrari and Quaresima 2012) {[}22{]}, and minimally invasive
  endovascular \texttt{Stentrode} arrays in the \texttt{SWITCH}
  paralysis trial (\texttt{n\ =\ 4} patients, \texttt{16}
  superior-sagittal-sinus electrodes, zero device-related serious
  adverse events at \texttt{12} months, Mitchell, Oxley et al.~2023)
  {[}38{]} prioritize safe readout at modest bitrates. Penetrating
  intracortical \texttt{Utah} microarrays and high-density
  \texttt{253}-channel subdural \texttt{ECoG} reach \texttt{90}
  characters/min handwriting (Willett et al.~2021) {[}50{]}, \texttt{62}
  words/min (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}15\ bit/s}) intracortical speech
  (Willett et al.~2023) {[}50{]}, and \texttt{78} words/min
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}19\ bit/s}) multimodal speech-and-avatar
  synthesis (Metzger et al.~2023) {[}36{]}, alongside
  \texttt{1,024+}-channel flexible polymer threads (Musk and Neuralink
  2019) {[}39{]}. Yet all of these channels remain
  \textbf{unidirectional externalizers} whose fluent text output relies
  heavily on external language-model priors and visual screen feedback.
\item
  \textbf{Single-brain sensory and therapeutic modulation (write-in and
  closed-loop, \texttt{BM04}, \texttt{BM05}, \texttt{BM09},
  \texttt{BM11} to \texttt{BM14})}: Mature clinical write-in exists for
  peripheral sensory prostheses: \texttt{CIS} cochlear implants
  (\texttt{\textgreater{}1,000,000} recipients,
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}20-40\ bit/s} speech reception in quiet,
  Wilson et al.~1991) and \texttt{Argus\ II} epiretinal arrays
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}1-5\ bit/s}, Humayun et al.~2012) {[}29{]}.
  Non-invasive cortical write-in spans diffuse
  \texttt{tDCS}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{tACS} excitability biasing (Nitsche and Paulus
  2000) {[}22{]} and millimeter-focal \texttt{tFUS} modulation of human
  \texttt{S1} (Legon et al.~2014) {[}33{]}, while millisecond
  cell-type-specific optogenetics (\texttt{BM11}, Boyden et al.~2005)
  {[}5{]} remains largely preclinical due to viral opsin delivery
  requirements. Crucially, closing the read-write loop inside a single
  human brain already multiplies functional agency (\texttt{{[}E{]}}):
  bidirectional \texttt{M1}-read plus \texttt{S1} tactile \texttt{ICMS}
  (\texttt{BM09}, Flesher et al.~2016, 2021) {[}23{]} cut robotic
  object-transfer times roughly in half
  (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}20.9\ s} down to
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}10.2\ s}), and blinded randomized crossover
  trials of chronic adaptive \texttt{DBS} in Parkinson's disease
  (\texttt{BM12}, Oehrn et al.~2024; Shanechi 2019) {[}43{]}
  outperformed open-loop continuous stimulation on symptom control.
\item
  \textbf{Proof-of-concept multi-brain relays versus predicted
  \texttt{N}-mind bridging (\texttt{BM15}, \texttt{BM16},
  \texttt{BM18})}: Existing brain-to-brain demonstrations, specifically
  rodent \texttt{ICMS} sensorimotor dyads (\texttt{BM15}, Pais-Vieira et
  al.~2013) {[}45{]} and human non-invasive \texttt{EEG}-to-\texttt{TMS}
  protocols culminating in three-person \texttt{BrainNet} collaborative
  \texttt{Tetris} (\texttt{BM16}, \texttt{81.25\%} accuracy across
  \texttt{5} triads, Rao et al.~2014; Jiang et al.~2019) {[}31{]},
  establish proof of principle (\texttt{{[}E{]}}) at ultra-low
  throughput (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}0.02} to \texttt{0.\allowbreak{}5\ bit/\allowbreak{}s},
  binary phosphene or conditioned cues). Scaling from
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}0.05\ bit/s} phosphenes to high-bandwidth
  semantic \texttt{N}-mind bridging (\texttt{BM18}, target
  \texttt{\textgreater{}39\ bit/s}) is strictly \textbf{speculative
  (\texttt{{[}S{]}})} and faces three hard barriers: inter-individual
  representational alignment (distinct cortical manifolds across
  brains), chronic high-channel read-write biocompatibility, and
  \textbf{agency preservation}. Theorems \texttt{CTA-T29} to
  \texttt{CTA-T31} formalize the non-negotiable architectural boundary:
  an agency-preserving bridge is \textbf{not} a shared global state
  overwrite; every participant \(i\) must retain a local,
  hardware-enforced opt-in gate \(g_i(s_i, m) \in \{0, 1\}\) that admits
  incoming latent packets into working perceptual/memory workspaces
  without bypassing volitional executive control, making opt-in bridging
  the mathematical inverse of forced assimilation (\texttt{CTA-T31}).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Worked Example 6: Consent-Gated
\texttt{N}-Mind Bridging vs.~Forced Assimilation
(\texttt{CTA-T29}--\texttt{CTA-T31})}{Worked Example 6: Consent-Gated N-Mind Bridging vs.~Forced Assimilation (CTA-T29--CTA-T31)}}\label{worked-example-6-consent-gated-n-mind-bridging-vs.-forced-assimilation-cta-t29cta-t31}

To make the formal distinction between \textbf{opt-in neural
supplementation} and \textbf{coercive assimilation} concrete
(\texttt{{[}T{]}} for gate logic; \texttt{{[}S{]}} for future high-rate
multi-brain hardware), trace a 3-participant mesh connecting human
operators \texttt{Alice} (\(i=1\)), \texttt{Bob} (\(i=2\)), and a
\texttt{Synthetic\ Co-\allowbreak{}Processor} (\(i=3\)):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Factored Participant State and Sovereignty Invariant}: Let
  each participant's state factor as
  \(s_i(t) = (\mathbf{g}_i(t),\, \mathbf{w}_i(t)) \in \{0,1\}^4 \times \mathbb{Q}^3\),
  where \(\mathbf{g}_i(t)\) encodes private volitional commitments
  (e.g., \texttt{Alice}'s baseline \(\mathbf{g}_1(0) = (1, 0, 1, 1)\))
  and \(\mathbf{w}_i(t)\) is an opt-in sensory/working-memory workspace
  \texttt{(\allowbreak{}slip\_\allowbreak{}force\_\allowbreak{}N,\allowbreak{}\ azimuth\_\allowbreak{}deg,\allowbreak{}\ confidence)}.
  \texttt{Alice}'s local autonomy invariant is:
  \[I_1(s_1) \iff \bigl(\mathbf{g}_1 = (1, 0, 1, 1)\bigr) \;\wedge\; \bigl(|q_{\text{stim}}| \le 30.0\;\mu\text{C/cm}^2\bigr).\]
  \texttt{Alice}'s hardware-enforced local gate evaluates
  \(g_1(s_1, m) = \mathbb{I}[\text{sig\_valid}(m) \wedge (\Delta\mathbf{g}(m) = \mathbf{0}) \wedge (|q_{\text{stim}}(m)| \le 30.0) \wedge (\text{consent\_switch}_1 = 1)]\).
\item
  \textbf{Trace Step \texttt{t\ =\ 0\ -\textgreater{}\ 1} (Admitted
  Collaborative Prosthetic Packet \(m_1\))}:
  \texttt{Synthetic\ Co-\allowbreak{}Processor} and \texttt{Bob} co-sign
  tactile-grasp packet
  \(m_1 = (\Delta\mathbf{g} = (0,0,0,0),\; \Delta\mathbf{w} = (+0.42, -15.0, +0.94),\; q_{\text{stim}} = 11.5\;\mu\text{C/cm}^2)\).
  \texttt{Alice}'s local gate verifies \texttt{BLAKE3+Ed25519}
  authenticity and checks
  \(I_1(\mathcal{T}(s_1(0), m_1)) = \text{true}\), outputting
  \(g_1(s_1(0), m_1) = 1\). Operator \texttt{bridgedStep} updates
  \texttt{Alice}'s perceptual workspace
  \(\mathbf{w}_1(1) = (0.42, -15.0, 0.94)\) while preserving
  \(\mathbf{g}_1(1) = (1, 0, 1, 1)\) identically (\texttt{CTA-T29}).
\item
  \textbf{Trace Step \texttt{t\ =\ 1\ -\textgreater{}\ 2} (Blocked
  Adversarial / Misaligned Pulse \(m_2\))}: Now inject adversarial or
  corrupted packet
  \(m_2 = (\Delta\mathbf{g} = (0, +1, 0, 0),\; \Delta\mathbf{w} = (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),\; q_{\text{stim}} = 44.0\;\mu\text{C/cm}^2)\)
  attempting both to flip volitional bit \(\mathbf{g}_1[1] : 0 \to 1\)
  and to exceed cortical charge-density limits:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Under Ungated Broadcast (\texttt{forcedStep},
    \texttt{CTA-T31})}: Without \(g_1\), state mutates immediately to
    \(\mathbf{g}_1^{\text{forced}}(2) = (1, 1, 1, 1) \ne \mathbf{g}_1(0)\)
    at \(44.0\;\mu\text{C/cm}^2\), falsifying \(I_1\) in
    \textbf{\texttt{1} message}.
  \item
    \textbf{Under Consent-Gated Bridging (\texttt{bridgedStep},
    \texttt{CTA-T29}, \texttt{CTA-T30}, proved with \texttt{0} Lean
    axioms)}: \texttt{Alice}'s local hardware interlock evaluates
    \(\Delta\mathbf{g}(m_2) \ne \mathbf{0}\) and
    \(q_{\text{stim}} > 30.0\), sets \(g_1(s_1(1), m_2) = 0\), clamps
    stimulation current to zero, and holds
    \(s_1(2) = s_1(1) = ((1,0,1,1),\, (0.42,-15.0,0.94))\) with
    \textbf{\texttt{0} autonomy-invariant violations} across arbitrary
    message sequences.
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{8.2 Preclinical versus clinical audit of
paracrine and \texttt{EV} neuro-supplementation (\texttt{EV01} to
\texttt{EV16})}{8.2 Preclinical versus clinical audit of paracrine and EV neuro-supplementation (EV01 to EV16)}}\label{preclinical-versus-clinical-audit-of-paracrine-and-ev-neuro-supplementation-ev01-to-ev16}

The handoff thesis also notes a biochemical parallel to computational
borrowing: \textbf{neurological supplementation}, in which compromised
cells ingest functional \texttt{mRNAs}, \texttt{miRNAs}, or metabolic
enzymes externalized via stem-cell secretomes or \texttt{EVs} rather
than relying solely on endogenous repair. Catalog
\texttt{openie-\allowbreak{}research-\allowbreak{}experiments/\allowbreak{}communication-\allowbreak{}avalanche/\allowbreak{}catalogs/\allowbreak{}secretome-\allowbreak{}ev-\allowbreak{}evidence-\allowbreak{}ledger.\allowbreak{}csv}
(\texttt{EV01} to \texttt{EV16}) audits 16 verified primary
peer-reviewed studies across kidney injury, cellular senescence,
ischemic stroke, \texttt{TBI}, epilepsy, \texttt{AD}, \texttt{PD}, and
monogenic/idiopathic autism models:

(\texttt{EV13}, Sanz-Ros et al.~2022) {[}49{]},
\texttt{ESC}-\texttt{EVs} carrying
\texttt{miR-\allowbreak{}15b-\allowbreak{}5p}/\texttt{miR-\allowbreak{}290a-\allowbreak{}5p} (\texttt{EV14}, Yu et al.~2023)
{[}49{]}, and young plasma \texttt{sEVs} upregulating mitochondrial
\texttt{PGC-\allowbreak{}1alpha} (\texttt{EV15}, \texttt{+12.4\%} mouse median
lifespan, Chen et al.~2024) {[}13{]} reverse senescence markers in mice.
& Pluripotent \texttt{ESC} \texttt{miR-290} cargoes carry oncogenic
proliferation risks; donor plasma/primary cell \texttt{sEV} lots vary in
potency; \textbf{zero Phase III randomized human anti-aging or
neurological rejuvenation trials} have established clinical efficacy. \\
\textbf{Stroke, epilepsy, \& \texttt{TBI} neural rescue (\texttt{EV02}
to \texttt{EV04}, \texttt{EV11}, \texttt{EV16})} & Systemic
\texttt{MSC}-exosomes promoted rat post-stroke neurovascular remodeling
(\texttt{EV02}, Xin et al.~2013) and \texttt{hNSC}-\texttt{EVs} reduced
lesion volume in gyrencephalic porcine stroke (\texttt{EV04}, Webb et
al.~2018) {[}41{]}. Intranasal \texttt{MSC} \texttt{A1}-exosomes curbed
hippocampal inflammation after status epilepticus (\texttt{EV03}, Long
et al.~2017) {[}34{]}, \texttt{hiPSC-NSC} \texttt{EVs} enhanced
neurogenesis (\texttt{EV11}, Upadhya et al.~2020) {[}34{]}, and
engineered/hypoxia-primed intranasal \texttt{BMSC}-\texttt{EVs}
accelerated mouse \texttt{TBI} recovery (\texttt{EV16}, Chen et
al.~2025; Li et al.~2025) {[}12{]}. & Rodent olfactory epithelium covers
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}50\%} of the nasal cavity versus
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}3\%-5\%} in adult humans, creating a steep
anatomical nose-to-brain dosing gap; intravenous boluses suffer heavy
hepatic/splenic clearance. \\
\textbf{Neurodegenerative \texttt{AD} / \texttt{PD} \& \texttt{BTBR} /
\texttt{Shank3B} autism models (\texttt{EV05}, \texttt{EV06},
\texttt{EV09}, \texttt{EV10}, \texttt{EV12})} & Intranasal
\texttt{SHED}-\texttt{EVs} normalized gait and nigrostriatal tyrosine
hydroxylase in \texttt{6-OHDA} \texttt{PD} rats (\texttt{EV06}, Narbute
et al.~2019) {[}41{]}; intranasal \texttt{MSC}-\texttt{EVs} increased
spine density in \texttt{3xTg} \texttt{AD} mice (\texttt{EV09}, Losurdo
et al.~2020) {[}35{]} and reduced plaque load/memory deficits in
\texttt{5XFAD} mice (\texttt{EV12}, Cone et al.~2021) {[}16{]}.
Intranasal \texttt{MSC}-exosomes rescued social interaction,
vocalizations, and repetitive grooming in \texttt{BTBR} (\texttt{EV05},
Perets et al.~2018) and \texttt{Shank3B} knockout mice (\texttt{EV10},
Perets et al.~2020) {[}47{]}. & Acute \texttt{6-OHDA} lesions and
transgenic \texttt{APP}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{PS1} overexpression do not recapitulate
sporadic human Lewy/tau progression; murine grooming/ultrasonic
vocalization shifts do \textbf{not} establish human language or
cognitive clinical benefit without \texttt{GMP} potency standards and
double-blind human \texttt{RCTs} (\texttt{{[}S{]}}). \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsection{9.2 Explicit scope limits and what is not
claimed}\label{explicit-scope-limits-and-what-is-not-claimed}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Strict hardware rail versus counter and cloud boundary
  (\texttt{board\_\allowbreak{}metered\ =\ true} strictly on
  \texttt{Jetson\ AGX\ Thor} in \texttt{CTA-E4}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{CTA-E5})}:
  Physical current-shunt \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}j} (\texttt{TI\ INA238}
  \texttt{VIN} and \texttt{INA3221} \texttt{VDD\_\allowbreak{}CPU\_\allowbreak{}SOC\_\allowbreak{}MSS}) is
  claimed solely for the CPU-only \texttt{NVIDIA\ Jetson\ AGX\ Thor}
  runs in \texttt{CTA-E4} and \texttt{CTA-E5} (with \texttt{A2}
  single-window idle subtraction withheld per \texttt{Falsifier\ 6} due
  to an active \texttt{GPU} co-tenant). All four remaining fleet hosts
  report \texttt{RAPL}, \texttt{ri\_\allowbreak{}energy\_\allowbreak{}nj}, or \texttt{NVML}
  \texttt{reported\_\allowbreak{}j}, \texttt{Tier\ 0} \texttt{LUT6} figures remain
  analytical \texttt{est\_j}, and remote cloud \texttt{LLM} server
  energy remains \texttt{not\_\allowbreak{}available}.
\item
  \textbf{No teleological biology or mother-tree anthropomorphism}:
  Evolutionary transitions (\texttt{CL01} to \texttt{CL13}) arise from
  natural selection on local biophysical interactions; following Karst,
  Jones, and Hoeksema (2023) {[}32{]}, common mycorrhizal carbon fluxes
  are not claimed as intentional parental messaging.
\item
  \textbf{No equivalence between \texttt{1}-bit \texttt{BrainNet}
  phosphenes and semantic mind-melding}: Existing multi-brain
  experiments (\texttt{BM15}, \texttt{BM16}) transmit
  \texttt{\textasciitilde{}0.02} to \texttt{0.\allowbreak{}5\ bit/\allowbreak{}s} binary cues;
  high-bandwidth \texttt{N}-mind bridging (\texttt{BM18}) remains an
  unproven future engineering horizon (\texttt{{[}S{]}}).
\item
  \textbf{No human clinical prescription or therapeutic efficacy claim
  for \texttt{EVs}/\allowbreak{}secretomes}: Preclinical rescues in
  \texttt{BTBR}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Shank3B}, \texttt{3xTg}/\allowbreak{}\texttt{5XFAD},
  \texttt{6-OHDA}, \texttt{MCAO}, and \texttt{TBI} animal models
  (\texttt{EV01} to \texttt{EV16}) do not establish safety, dosing, or
  clinical benefit in human patients.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{9.3 Conclusion}\label{conclusion}

From \texttt{3.8\ Ga} extracellular vesicles and \texttt{3.5\ Ga}
cooperative quorum switches to \texttt{SepJ} cyanobacterial nanopores,
\texttt{Dictyostelium} \texttt{cAMP} relays, choanoflagellate
\texttt{VGCC} calcium waves, starling topological flocks, human
print/network archives, and sparse \texttt{MoE} / multi-agent
verification societies, exponential jumps in collective capability
follow one structural invariant: \textbf{externalize state into a shared
substrate, gate ingestion on cooperative thresholds and constant-time
verification, and lock verified discoveries into persistent shared
memory so downstream generations inherit competence without re-paying
the full search energy}. Formalizing that invariant in
\texttt{Lean\ 4.\allowbreak{}36.\allowbreak{}0} (\texttt{45} kernel-checked theorems
\texttt{CTA-T1} to \texttt{CTA-T45}), mapping \texttt{76} years of
artificial intelligence (\texttt{AI01} to \texttt{AI38}) onto the same
operator, and executing pre-registered simulations (\texttt{CA-M1} to
\texttt{CA-M5}) alongside five-host physical-shunt and counter
benchmarks (\texttt{CTA-E1} to \texttt{CTA-E5}) turns an evolutionary
synthesis into three testable engineering specifications: immediate
ten-paradigm Digital Enzymatic Synthesis (\texttt{DES-10}, \texttt{98\%}
verified pass at \texttt{9.26x}--\texttt{27.13x} higher \texttt{CpJ}),
closed-loop four-tier downward compilation across month, quarter, year,
and decade horizons (\texttt{HM01} to \texttt{HD04}), and
hardware-enforced opt-in agency gates (\(g_i = 1\)) that separate future
collaborative neurotechnology from forced assimilation.
